Customizing and Running the Budget Report 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > General Ledger > All > Reports > Financial Statements > Budget`


To customize the Budget report: 

1. 
Open the General Ledger menu.  

2. 
In the Reports column, in the Financial statements section, select Budget.  

Filling In the Time Period Section 

Define the time period for the report using either a budget period or date range. 

To run a report for a budget period: 

• 
In the Budget period drop-down list, select the Budget period. 

To run a report for a date range: 

• 
Specify the starting and ending dates to identify the report’s period of coverage.  
Type “ p ”in a date field to automatically insert the last day of the prior month. See 
Specifying Dates in Getting Started for a full list of shortcut keys related to entering dates.

---


<!-- Page 147 -->
Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Filling In the Filters Section 

1. 
Select the Budget type for the report. 

2. 
In the G/L account number selection section, select how you want to filter account numbers: 

• 
Range: Select this option to select a contiguous set of account numbers. Select the 

beginning and ending account numbers in the Account from and Account to 

drop-down lists. 

• 
Multiple accounts: Select this option if you want to select multiple, non-contiguous 

account numbers, and then click the Select accounts link to open the Select multiple 

accounts box. For more information, see Using the Select Multiple Accounts Box (on 

page 306). 

• 
All accounts: Select this option to include all account numbers in the report. 

3. 
In the Account group drop-down list, select an account group to filter by. You can also click 

the Find link to search for the desired account group. 

4. 
Select the Include statistical accounts in output check box if you want to include statistical 

account data in the Budget report. Clear the check box to exclude statistical account data. 

5. 
In the Segments drop-down list, select one or more segments to include in the report. For 

more information, see Using the multi-select box. 

6. 
In the Dimension filters section, select the dimensions that you want to use in the report. 

7. 
To filter by dimension report structure, select the desired dimension report structure in the 

Dimension report structure drop-down list. 

The next few drop-down lists are dimensions such as Department, Location, Customer, and 

Vendor. 

8. 
Select the desired dimension or dimension group to include dimensions in the report. To 

limit the report to a selected property (location) or location group, click the Select button 

under the Property label, and then select a property or group from the Selection box. After 

choosing a couple of properties, the Select button is replaced with the list of the properties 

that you chose: 

9. 
Select the Include subs check box if you want to include data from children of the selected 

location. 

10. 
Some dimensions have a type, such as a Customer type or Vendor type. Select the type 

when you want to run the report and include only one specific type of customer or vendor.

Setup 
tab. You can add and remove Vendor types in the Accounts Payable application’ s Setup 
tab. 

Filling In the Format Section 

1. 
Click the Show dimension values’ Select button, and then select the dimension values that 

you want to include in the report.  

If a user has already selected dimension values, the Show dimension values box appears 

instead of a Select button. For more information, see Using the multi-select box. 

2. 
In the Place total column drop-down list, choose where you want to display the Total 

column in relation to the budget period columns. Select to display it on the Right or Left of 

the budget period columns, or choose Hide. 

3. 
Select the Include notes column(s) for period(s) check box to include notes you’ve created 

for budget periods. Create notes in the Budget Account Information page. See Adding 

budget details for an account (on page 144).

budget model from OneSite and selected the Include Comments check box on the

Modelspage.

CSV file. If you select this check box, all available dimension values will be exported, and 

totals will not be included. Use the filters to customize your CSV file. When exporting, select 

CSV from the Export drop-down list. 

5. 
Click the View button to run the report.

Budget Report Columns 

Click any link to navigate into the details. The default Budget report contains the following 

columns: 

Columns 

Budget 
This is the name of the budget. 

Account 
This column contains the account numbers in the budget. 

Account name 
This column contains the account name. 

Location name 
This column contains the name of the location associated with the 

account. 

Department name 
This column contains the name of the department associated with the 

account. 

Currency 
This column contains the currency used in the budget. 

This column displays only if your company is set up to handle multiple currencies.

Total 
This is the amount in the budget for the account and the budget period. 

Month Ended 
This column indicates the budget period. 

This column appears only when you have selected a budget period 

range. 

Note-Month ended 
This is a note associated with the account for the budget period. 

Buttons 

Customize 
Opens the report’s customization page to change filters and time periods 

and to exclude specific types of data. 

The Customize button appears only after viewing a report or ledger.

View 
Opens the report in your browser. 

Print 
Displays the report in printable PDF format so you can print it or save it on 

your computer. 

Process & store 
Generates the report and stores it for later access. 

Available formats include HTML, printable (PDF), Excel, CSV 

(comma-separated value), and Text. You can store reports in the system, 

and then access them with the My stored reports option in the menu.  

---


<!-- Page 150 -->
Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Email 
Sends the report as an email message. Click to open the Email report box, 

and then fill in the To field with the recipient’s email address. 
This button is not available for all reports.

Add to dashboard 
Adds the report to a dashboard. 

Select the dashboard that you want to add it to, type a name for the 

report, and then type a description of the report.  

Select the Public check box to make the report available on a dashboard. 
This option is not available for all reports. 

Memorize 
Memorizes (that is, saves) the report in the system so you can refer to it 

later or modify and re-run it. 
Reports that you memorize can be scheduled to run on a recurring basis and can be automatically emailed.

Export 
Exports the report to a separate file that you can then distribute or save on 

your computer. 
The file formats for exporting report data vary by report.

Select 
Opens the multi-select box, where you can select items to filter by. 

In the multi-select box, select the items that you want to include in the 

report.  
The option to multi-select items is not available for all reports.

Use the Budget details list to manage the budgets that you have created for every 

account-department-location combination. From this page, you can add, edit, and delete 

budgets. 

In addition, you can click the Budgets link to display a list of the period data and other budget 

information for an account. If your list of budgets is long, use the sort and filter features of the list 

to select those that you want to edit. 
There are two ways to add account- level data to a budget. You can manually enter the data, or 
you can upload a spreadsheet.

##### Adding Budget Details for an Account
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 154*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Adding Budget Details for an Account 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > General Ledger > All > Budgets > Budget Details > Add`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Budgets > Budget Details > Add`


Use the Budget account information page to add or edit the account for which you are 

budgeting, the dimensions for the account, the periods for the account budget, and the budget 

amount for each period. 

To add budget detail for an account: 

1. 
In the Budgets list, click the Budget details link next to the account for which you want to 

add budget details. The Budget details list opens. 

2. 
Click the Add button. The Budget amount details page opens.  

3. 
In the Account drop-down list, select the account for which you want to add a budget. 

4. 
Optionally, from the dimension drop-down lists (department, location, customer, vendor), 

click the dimensions to add to the budget.  
After you save the budget detail, you can’t change the dimension settings.

Additional Rules for Department and Location 

Calculations are based on the combination of account, department, and location. In other 

words, if you specify the account 6020 Freight, the department Operations and the location 

California, the calculations are based on numbers only for that account and department 

combination that is also located in California.  

All children of parent departments and locations are included in the calculations. You might 

want to budget at the child level, and not budget at the parent level, for example. This 

enables you to track costs and revenues to budget, at a very detailed level. For this reason, 

we recommend that you plan your budget before you begin entering, such as preparing 

your budget first on a separate piece of paper or Excel spreadsheet, for faster entry into the 

system.  

5. 
Filter by Segment type and Segment value. 
If the Segment type and 
Segment value fields do not appear, then your system may not 
have the segments feature enabled(on page 407).

6. 
To select the reporting periods for your budget: 

• 
Click the 
 Calendar button in the Periods from date field, and then select the 

starting date.  

• 
Click the 
 Calendar button in the Periods to date field, and then select the ending 

date.  

---


<!-- Page 155 -->
Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

These dates represent the period for which you are preparing the budget. Typically, this 

covers a fiscal or calendar year.  


> **Navigation Path:** `7. 
Click Show periods to display the reporting periods in the range you just specified. 
When you click the Show periodsbutton, if no period
s display, go to Reports > All > More`


> Reporting Periods. Click the 
Edit link next to the reporting period that you want to use, 
and then select the Use for budgeting check box.

 If the period has been selected for use in another existing budget 
combination of reporting period, account, department, and location, it is not saved. An error message appears informing you that it is already in use.

8. 
Click the Select check box for the reporting periods to add to your budget, and then assign 

budget amounts to those periods. You can either enter the amounts manually or have them 

computed for you. 

9. 
To enter them manually, enter them into the End of period budget amount fields. 

To automatically compute budget amounts: 

1. 
Click the Past period drop-down list, and then select the monthly period to use. 

2. 
Select the desired method from the Based on drop-down list: 

• 
Actuals. Selecting Actuals retrieves the actuals for the selected period. You do not 

need to enter any information in the Compute amount field. 

• 
Budget. To base your budget on a fixed value, enter that amount in the Compute 

amount field. 

• 
Employee count. The number you enter in the Compute amount field is multiplied by 

the number of employees to populate the Budget amount field. The employee list is 

used in budgets so that budgets can be based on head count. More specifically, the 

calculation is performed from the start date to the end date of employment, which you 

can check via Company > Employees. A budget calculation based on head count 

retrieves data for all employees who were in active status during the selected period. 

Thus, it is important that your employee list be current if you plan to use this function 

in your budgeting process. 

3. 
Type a number in the Grow by field. This number can be a positive or negative (or zero) 

amount or a percentage. 

4. 
Select either the Percent or Amount option, depending on how you want the budget 

growth to be based. 

5. 
Type a starting amount in the Compute amount field. 

6. 
Select the check box of each line item to which you want to apply the results. 

---

7. 
Click the Compute button. 

8. 
Click Save. After you save a budget, you can’t change the account or any dimensions 

included. 

Editing Budget Details for a Specific Account 

After a budget for an account is saved, you can edit: 

• 
Period from and period to dates 

• 
Based on, grow by, and compute amount 

If you assigned the amount to a particular dimension such as department, item, or employee and 

determine that the wrong dimension was assigned, you have two choices: 

• 
Reset the budget amount to zero (0). 

• 
Delete the old account budget and create a new account budget with the correct dimension 

assignments. You need only delete the budget period for the account, not the entire 

overarching budget or budgets for other accounts. 

To edit a budget’s details: 

1. 
From the Applications menu, select the General Ledger option, and then select the All tab. 

2. 
Select Budgets. 

3. 
Locate the budget that you want to edit, and then click the Budget details link. The Budget 

details list opens. 

4. 
Find the desired account that you want to change, and then click the Edit link. The Budget 

amount details page opens. 

5. 
Make the desired changes. Refer to the Fields (on page 153) topic for field-by-field 

descriptions. 

6. 
Click Save.

##### Entering Budget Amounts for the Reporting Periods
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 162*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Entering Budget Amounts for the Reporting Periods 

Entering Amounts Manually 

1. 
Select the check box to the left of the Reporting period to activate it. 

2. 
Type amounts into the Budget amount fields for each budget period. 

3. 
Optionally, add a note about the budget or budget amount. 

4. 
After the data has been entered for this account, click Save. 

Automatically Compute Budget Data Based on Amounts from a Past Period 

1. 
Select the period you want to use as the starting point for the calculation from the Past 

periods drop-down list. 

2. 
Select a method from the Based on drop-down list. You can choose to base the calculation 

on Actual data, Budget data, or Employee Count. The three choices are described in the 

following table. 

Based on 

method 

Description 

Actuals 
This method retrieves the actual amount for the selected Past period. Budget values 

are calculated by changing the actual value for the selected Past period by the 

amount or percentage entered in the Grow by field. The Compute amount field is 

not required. 

Budget 
This method retrieves the budget amount for the selected Past period. Budget 

values are calculated by changing the Past period value by the amount or 

percentage entered in the Grow by field. The Compute amount field is not required. 

Employee Count You can use the Employees list in budgets so that budgets can be based on head 

count. It is important that your Employees list be current if you plan to use this 

function in your budgeting process. 

The number that you enter in the Compute amount field is multiplied by the 

number of employees to populate the Budget amount field. 

The calculation is performed based on the number of active employees, based on 

the start date and the end date of employment.  

To view start and end dates, go to Company > All tab > More > Dimensions > 

Employees. 

A budget calculation based on employee head count retrieves data for all employees 

without an end date as of the selected Past period. 

To add reporting periods to your budget: 

1. 
Select the check box for the Reporting periods in the lower part of the page.

Click Save.

Based on

• 
Actuals: The budget is based on the actuals for the selected 

period. 

• 
Budget: The budget is based on the amount entered in the 

Compute amount field.

the Compute amount field multiplied by the number of 

employees.

Setting Up a Budget Check As Part of Approvals 

As part of the approval process, you can have the system check the default budget against the 

amount of a transaction to see if it will exceed the budget. The budget checks A/P invoices and 

purchase orders. You can either have the system prevent the user from creating an A/P invoice or 

purchase order that exceeds the budget or display a warning when the user tries to save the 

transaction that exceeds the budget. 

Configuring Budget Checking 

To allow the system to perform the budget checks: 

1. 
Open the Company application from the Applications menu.  

2. 
On the All tab, in the Approvals section, select Approval Configuration. 

3. 
To allow the system to prevent or warn users when they create an A/P invoice that exceeds 

the budgeted amount, go to the A/P invoice approvals section, and then select the Enable 

preventing or warning a user when adding an AP Invoice that exceeds the budget check 

box. 

4. 
In the Purchase Orders section, select the Enable preventing or warning a user when 

adding a Purchase Order that exceeds the budget check box. 

5. 
In the Budget Check section, the system uses the budget check Duration setting in the 

approval configuration to determine whether to use the amount budgeted for the current 

month, for the current year, or for the year-to-date.  

You can also assign a user permission to bypass budget check for one or both transaction types. 

---



##### Setting a System Action When a Transaction Exceeds the Budget
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 166*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Setting a System Action When a Transaction Exceeds the Budget 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Company > All > Approvals > Approval Configuration`


Use this procedure to determine what happens when an A/P invoice or purchase order exceeds 

the budgeted amount.  

To set up the system action: 

1. 
In the Applications menu, select the Company application, and then click the All tab. 

2. 
In the Approvals section, select the Approval Configuration menu option. 

The Approval configuration page opens. 

3. 
To enable the budget warning or prevention for A/P invoices: 

• 
Find the A/P Invoice Approvals section. 

• 
Select the Enable preventing or warning a user when adding an AP Invoice that 

exceeds the budget check box. 

• 
To have the system display a warning message that the user is about to exceed the 

budgeted amount by posting an invoice, select Warn. 

• 
To have the system prevent the user from posting the invoice, select Prevent. The 

system both prevents the invoice from posting and displays the warning message. 

4. 
To enable the budget warning or prevention for purchase orders: 

• 
Find the Purchase orders section. 

• 
Select the Enable preventing or warning a user when adding a Purchase Order that 

exceeds the budget check box. 

• 
To have the system display a warning message that the user is about to exceed the 

budgeted amount by the purchase order, select Warn. 

• 
To have the system prevent the user from posting the purchase order, select Prevent. 

The system both prevents the purchase order from posting and displays the warning 

message. 

5. 
Find the Budget check section, and then review the Default duration setting: 

• 
Current month: Select this option to use the amount budgeted for the G/L account for 

the current posting month. 

• 
Current year: Select this option to use the amount budgeted for the G/L account for 

the current posting year. 

• 
Current year through current month: Select this option to use the amount budgeted 

for the G/L account for the year to date. 

6. 
Click Save. 

---

Working Budgets List 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Flipview`


Use the Working budgets list to view the working budgets that you have created for your 

properties.  

A working budget is a draft of a budget. When budget figures are agreed to, you commit the 

budget. After committing a working budget, you can access the information in the Budgets list. 

You can use working budget data in a Financial report by selecting “Working Budget” in the 

Columns drop-down list of the Financial report writer. 

Budget by Property View 

The list offers a second view called Budget by property. Use the Budget by property view to 

search a budget by property, budget ID, and year.

To view the alternate view, click the Flip view button.  

Before You Begin 

Access can vary depending on your organization’s subscriptions and your permissions. If you do 

not see the option in the menu, then ask your System Administrator to check your permissions. 

Permissions 

These permissions are required to work with working budgets. 

Application 
Activities/Lists 
Permissions 

Forecasting & Planning 
Working Budgets 
List, View, Add, Edit, Delete, Publish 

If your user account doesn’t have the permissions shown above, then ask your System 

Administrator for assistance. If you have administrator privileges, you can assign permissions for a 

new feature to a user or to a role. See Roles and Permissions for a description of how to review a 

user’s permissions. 

Before you try to add a budget for a future timeframe, make sure that the system has reporting 

periods for that time. To create reporting periods, you must have access to the Reports 

application’s Setup tab. See Adding reporting periods.

Adding a Working Budget 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > 
 Working Budget`


You can add a working budget for one or more properties. 

To add a working budget: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the 
 Add button next to Working budgets.  

The Working budget page opens. 

3. 
In the Budget field, type a unique name for the budget that you are creating. 

4. 
Select the method of creating a budget that you want to use: 

• 
Create a new budget: Select this option to create a new budget for one or more 

properties. 

• 
Create a budget from working budget and reporting period range: Select this option 

to create the new budget based on an existing working budget, and then select the 

working budget. 

Create a budget from G/L budget and reporting period range: Select this option to 
create a working budget based on an existing G/L budget. Select the desired G/L 
budget to base the new budget on. 

5. 
In the first drop-down list, select the G/L budget to base the new working budget on.  

6. 
In the Source year drop-down list, select the year that the information is coming from. 

7. 
In the Create for year drop-down list, select the year the working budget is being defined 

for. 

8. 
In the Start fiscal month drop-down list, select the reporting period for the start of the 

budget period. 

9. 
In the End fiscal month drop-down list, select the reporting period for the end of the 

budget period. 
You can select Accounts and 
Account groupsonly when creating a new budget from 
scratch.You cannot add or remove accounts to a new working budget that is based on another budget (fromeither another working budget or a budget in the General Ledger).When you create a working budget from another budget, then the system uses all of the accounts from the source budget.

10. 
In the Accounts section, select one of the options: 

• 
All accounts: Select this option to include all accounts in the budget.

*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 171*

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

• 
Select account group: Select this option if you want to create the new budget with the 

accounts in an account group. If you select this option, the Account group drop-down 

list appears for you to select the desired account group.  

11. 
In the Property drop-down list, select the property or multiple properties that you are 

creating the budget for. For more information, see Using the Multi-Select Box. 

The Status field displays either Working or Published. The default status is Working. 

12. 
Click Save. You can also click the More actions button and then select Save and new to 

save the current working budget and then create a new working budget. 

Editing a Working Budget 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details > Edit`


To edit a working budget: 

1. 
In the Working budgets list, click the Budget details link for the budget that you want to 

edit.  

The Working budget summary page opens. 

2. 
Click the Edit link next to the account whose budget details you want to edit. 

The Account detail page opens.  

3. 
Click the 
 Expand button to see the page.

• 
Department 

• 
Customer 

• 
Employee

If the Segment type and 
Segment value drop
-down lists do not appear, then your system 
does not have the segments feature enabled(on page 407).

Adding Itemization Rows from Other Budgets  

1. 
In the Budget drop-down list, select the budget. The system completes the year in the Year 

field. 

2. 
Click the Apply button. The system completes the amounts in the Itemization rows section.  

3. 
In the Itemization rows section, change the budget amounts as needed. You can add 

amounts on a single line or add amounts on multiple lines if you want the budget total for 

the account to come from the Total line.

<!-- Page 172 -->
Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Having the System Calculate Budget Increases  

You can update the budget amounts for this account by having the system calculate them for 

you. You can either specify a percentage or a dollar amount to grow the budget by. 

To increase each budget period by a specific amount: 

1. 
Select the row that you want to change. 

2. 
Select Amount in the Based on section. 

3. 
In the Grow by field, type the amount that you want to increase the budget each period. 

4. 
In the Compute amount field, type the starting amount for the budget line. 

5. 
Click the Compute button. 

This example shows a row that starts with $1350.00 and grows by $12 each budget period. 

To grow each budget period by a percentage: 

1. 
Select the row that you want to change. 

2. 
Select Percent in the Based on section. 

3. 
In the Grow by field, type the percentage by which you want to increase the budget each 

period. 

4. 
In the Compute amount field, type the starting amount for the budget line. 

5. 
Click the Compute button. 

---

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

This example shows a row that starts with $1350.00 and grows by 10% each budget period. 

Completing the Changes 

After adjusting the budget amounts to your satisfaction, you can: 

• 
Add a comment in the Comments section. 

• 
Click the More actions button and then select Next or Previous to continue working 

on budget amounts for other accounts.  

• 
Click Save. When the Working budget summary page opens, click the Refresh button 

to see the changes to the account’s budget row, and the Total row at the bottom of 

the budget. 

Viewing a Working Budget 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > View`


You can view a working budget’s timeframe, property, and status, and publishing history on the 

Working budget page. 

To view a working budget’s top facts: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the View link of the desired working budget. 

The Working budget page opens. 
If needed, you can duplicate the budget’s information to start working on a new one.

Deleting or Inactivating a Working Budget 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Delete`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Inactivate`


You can access budgets from the General Ledger application and the Forecasting & Planning 

application. This topic describes how to delete a budget in the Forecasting & Planning 

application. 

You can delete a working budget permanently or inactivate it to remove it from the Working 

budgets list and system drop-down lists. 

To delete a working budget: 

1. 
In the Working budgets list, click the Delete link next to the working budget you want to 

delete. 

A confirmation box opens. 

2. 
Click OK to confirm. 

To inactivate a working budget: 

1. 
In the Working budgets list, click the Inactivate link next to the working budget you want 

to inactivate. 

A confirmation box opens. 

2. 
Click OK to confirm. 

The working budget is hidden from the list and will no longer be available in system 

drop-down lists. 
To reactivate an inactive budget, select the Include inactivecheck box at the top of the 
Working budgetslist, and then click the 
Reactivate link next to the working budget.

#### Advance Actuals Box
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 178*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Advance Actuals Box 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > View > Advance 
Actuals`


Use the Advance actuals box to report year-to-date with the forecasted (budgeted) amounts for 

the remainder of the year. For example, if you are going into May 1st, you can advance your 

actuals through April of the current year. Doing this will pull all actual amounts from January 

through April into the working budget, and May through December will report budgeted 

amounts. 
You can advance actualsin bulk for multiple properties in the same working budget.

---

*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 179*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Advancing Year-to-Date Actuals 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > View > Advance 
Actuals`


To advance actuals through the previous budget period and report year-to-date actuals in your 

working budget: 

1. 
On the Working budget page, click the Advance actuals button. 

2. 
The Advance actuals box opens. 

3. 
In the Actuals through drop-down list, select the budget period through which you are 

advancing your actuals; for example, the budget period ending in the previous month. 

4. 
In the Reporting book drop-down list, select the book type for which you are reporting 

actuals, either “Accrual” or “Cash.” 

5. 
Select the check box next to the property or properties for which you are advancing actuals. 

6. 
Click the Advance button. 

The system advances the actual amounts in the working budget through the end of selected 

reporting period.

Working Budget Summary Page 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details`


The Working budget summary page displays a list of G/L accounts associated with a working 

budget, including their budget periods and amounts. Expand the account group to view the 

budget amounts of each itemization row and the totals for each budget period. 

You can edit, view, and delete a G/L account’s budget row. The workflow is as follows: 

1. 
Publish the working budget (on page 178). 

2. 
Add a row for a G/L account to the working budget (on page 171). 

3. 
Importing budget data (on page 174). 

• 
Download (on page 174) the Budgets_.xls template. 

• 
Import (on page 175) budget data from a CSV file. 

• 
Export the working budget to a file. 

4. 
Refresh the working budget with the latest data, and then update the totals. 

5. 
View publishing history (on page 164) of a working budget.

Downloading or Exporting a Template for Importing Budget Data 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details > 
Template`


There are two ways to get a CSV template for importing data into a working budget: 

• 
Download the default, blank template. 

• 
Export a Working Budget report as an importable CSV template. 

Downloading a Blank Template 

To download a blank template for importing working budget details: 

1. 
In the Forecasting & Planning application menu, select the All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the Working budgets option.  

---


<!-- Page 185 -->
Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

The Working budgets list opens. 

3. 
Click the Budget details link of the desired working budget.  

The Working budget summary list opens. 

4. 
Click the Template button.  

The system downloads the template to your browser’s default downloads folder. 

5. 
Open the file in Excel, and then fill it with your budget data. 

6. 
When finished, save the file in CSV format. 

Exporting a Working Budget Report as an Import Template 

To export a Working Budget report as an importable CSV template: 

1. 
In the Forecasting & Planning application menu, select the All tab. 

2. 
In the Reports section, click the Working budget report option. 

The Working budget report page opens. 

3. 
Customize the report (on page 303) as needed. Be sure to select the Use CSV importable 

format check box in the Format section. 

4. 
Click View to run the report. 

5. 
Click the Export drop-down button, and then select the CSV option from the drop-down 

list. 

The report data will be exported to an import-compatible CSV file. You can add or edit the 

budget details as needed and then import the file back into the system to update the 

working budget.

Importing Data into a Working Budget 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details > 
Import`


To import data from the CSV file into a budget: 

1. 
On the Working budget summary page, click the Import button.

also select it and then click the box’s Open button. 

4. 
In the Date format, select the date format that you used for entering dates in the CSV file. 

5. 
Leave the File encoding setting as is. 

6. 
Select the Process offline check box when you want the system to run the report when you 

are offline. Choose this option when you want to prevent your report from affecting system 

performance. 

7. 
In the Email results to this address field, type your email address. 

8. 
Click the Import button. 
If your import has errors, see Troubleshooting Budget Import Errors(on page 133).

---



##### Troubleshooting Budget Import Errors
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 187*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Troubleshooting Budget Import Errors 

There are several reasons why a budget upload might fail. The following are some of the common 

error messages you might encounter, with the steps to resolve them. 

Error 
Steps to resolve 

G/L account not valid 
Verify that you have entered the account number correctly and 

that the account has been created in the General Ledger. 

Duplicate budget 

accounts for a period 

Verify that only a single amount has been entered by period for 

each G/L account/dimension combination. 

For example, if you are budgeting by G/L account only, then you 

cannot have two rows for account 4010. If you are budgeting by 

account and dimension, then you cannot have amounts in two 

rows for the same account/dimension combination. 

Incorrect, not budgetable 

or missing reporting 

periods 

Verify that your reporting periods are correct and that the 

reporting periods are marked as budgetable. 

The reporting periods in your import file must exactly match the 

reporting periods in the system. 

To make a reporting period budgetable, edit the reporting period, 

and then select the Use for budgeting option. 
The dates of periods selected for budgets cannot overlap. For example, you have two reporting periods: one includes January 1 to January 31, and another includes January 15 to February 15. If you select the first to be used for budgeting, then you can’ t 
select the second.

Invalid budget ID 
Verify that the Budget ID has been created and that the value 

entered in the Budget_ID column exactly matches the Budget ID in 

the system. 

---



##### Exporting a Working Budget
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 188*



##### Publishing a Working Budget
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 188*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Exporting a Working Budget 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details > 
Export`


From the Working budget summary page, you can export the data to a spreadsheet. 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the Working budgets option. The Working budgets list 

opens. 

3. 
Click the Budget details link of the desired working budget. The Working budget summary 

list opens. 

4. 
Click the Export button. The Downloads list opens with the most current exported data at 

the top of the list. 

5. 
Select the Open file link to open the spreadsheet that you exported.  

Publishing a Working Budget 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details > 
Publish`


Publish a working budget when you finalized your changes and do not expect to make further 

changes. After publishing a working budget, the status changes to Published.  

You can still make changes to the working budget after publishing it. Just change the same 

working budget and publish it again to update it.  

To publish a budget:  

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the Working budgets option. The Working budgets list 

opens. 

3. 
Click the Budget details link of the desired working budget. The Working budget summary 

list opens. 

4. 
Click the Publish button. The Publish working budget page displays. 

5. 
In the Publish to drop-down list, select the name of the budget that you want to publish the 

working budget as. The budget names in the list come from the Budgets list.  

6. 
In the Property list, select the property or properties to associate with the budget. Click the 

top check box to select all properties. 

---


<!-- Page 189 -->
Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

If you are still working to finalize a budget for one or more properties, you can exclude the property’s budgets from being
published until you have completed them. Clear a property’s check box to exclude the property from the published budget.

 The history of this working budget appears in the
Publish historysection at the bottom of the page. When you are republishing a working budget, check the Publish historysection to see which properties were previously assigned the budget. Consider selecting these properties as you republish the budget.

7. 
Click the Publish button. The working budget’s status changes from Working to Published, 

and the budget appears in the Budgets list with the name. 

8. 
Click Done. 

---

#### Account Detail Page
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 191*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Account Detail Page 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details > 
Working Budget Summary > Edit or View`


Use the Account Detail page to view and change budget data for one G/L account. This page is 

divided into three sections: 

• 
The top section offers filters and controls that determine what appears in the Itemization 

rows list in the second section. 

• 
The Itemization rows section contains a list of budget period amounts for each account line 

item. 

• 
The bottom section contains a text comment field. 

From this page, you can: 

• 
Add budget amounts for one account. 

• 
Remove a row of budget amounts. 

• 
Import budget data for one account. 

• 
Adjust the budget amounts of one or more account line items. 

• 
Add a comment about this account’s budget amounts. 

• 
Calculate a growth amount based on an amount or a percentage. 

• 
Filter by dimension or segment. 

---



##### Adding Budget Amounts for One Account
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 192*



##### Importing Budget Data for One Account

Adding Budget Amounts for One Account 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details > 
Working Budget Summary > Edit or View`


You can add budget data to an account manually. 

To add data manually: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the Working Budgets option. The Working Budget list 

opens. 

3. 
Click the Budget details link of the desired budget. The Working Budget Summary page 

opens. 

4. 
Find the account that you want to add budget amounts to, and then click the Edit link. The 

Account details page opens. 

5. 
In the Itemization rows section, type the amounts in the account’s Budget row.  

You can also add more rows by clicking the 
 Add button at the end of the row.  

When you have multiple lines for an account, the system uses the total from the Total row 

in the Itemization rows section for the account. 

6. 
In the Comments field, type comments about the budgeted amounts or the account as 

needed. 

7. 
Click the Save button. 

Importing Budget Data for One Account 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details > 
Working Budget Summary > Edit or View`


To add budget data to the account from another budget: 

1. 
From the Account details page, select the budget from the Budget drop-down list.  

2. 
Click the Apply button. The system completes the Year field and fills in the Itemization rows 

list with budget amounts from the budget that you selected.

##### Calculating the Budget Increase for an Account
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 193*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Calculating the Budget Increase for an Account 

You can update the budget amounts for this account by having the system calculate them for 

you. You can either specify a percentage or a dollar amount to grow the budget by. 

To increase each budget period by a specific amount: 

1. 
Select the row that you want to change. 

2. 
Select Amount in the Based on section. 

3. 
In the Grow by field, type the amount that you want to increase the budget each period. 

4. 
In the Compute amount field, type the starting amount for the budget line. 

5. 
Click the Compute button. 

This example shows a row that starts with $1350.00 and grows by $12 each budget period. 

To grow each budget period by a percentage: 

1. 
Select the row that you want to change. 

2. 
Select Percent in the Based on section. 

3. 
In the Grow by field, type the percentage by which you want to increase the budget each 

period. 

4. 
In the Compute amount field, type the starting amount for the budget line. 

5. 
Click the Compute button. 

---



##### Adjusting a Working Budget by Account
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 194*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

This example shows a row that starts with $1350.00 and grows by 10% each budget period. 

Adjusting a Working Budget by Account 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details > 
(Account) Edit`


You can adjust the working budget of an account at the line-item level from the Account detail 

page. To adjust working budgets in bulk for multiple properties and accounts, see 
Adjusting a Working Budget in Bulk(on page 187).

To make an adjustment for an individual account: 

1. 
On the Working budget summary page, find the desired account, and then click the Edit 

link. 

The Account detail box opens. 

2. 
In the Itemization rows section, select one or more line items. 

3. 
In the Percent adjustment field, type the percentage by which you are adjusting the budget 

amounts. The Percent adjustment increases the budgeted amount for each budget period 

by the percentage specified; for example, if the budget for a period is $15,000, and you 

adjust it by 2.5% (expressed in the system as 2.50), then the budget will update to 

$15,375.00. 

4. 
Click the Compute button. 

The line item(s) update with the adjusted budget amounts and totals. You can change the 

Percent adjustment as needed before saving the adjustment. 

5. 
Click Save to save the adjustment and return to the Working budget summary page. 
Adjustments made at the account level do not appear in the Adjustment historysection of the Adjustment box.

---

Filtering by Dimension or Segment 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details > 
Working Budget Summary > Edit`


You can filter the account budget data by a dimension or by a segment. 

Filtering By Dimension 

A common application of filtering by a dimension would be to filter by department to see the 

budgeted amount for a specific department. 

To filter the budget amounts for the account by dimension: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the Working Budgets option. The Working Budget list 

opens. 

3. 
Click the Budget details link of the desired budget. The Working Budget Summary page 

opens. 

4. 
Find the desired dimension field (Department, Customer, or Employee), and then select the 

desired department, customer, or employee to filter on. 

Filtering by Segment 

To filter the budget amounts for the account by segment: 

1. 
If your system has segments enabled, the Segment type and Segment value fields appear. 

If they do not, you must enable segments, and then define your company’s segments. See 

---

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Enabling segments (on page 407). After enabling segments, you must add segment types 

and segment values. See Adding segments (on page 409). 

2. 
In the Segment type drop-down list, select the desired segment type that you want to filter 

on. 

3. 
In the Segment value drop-down list, select the desired segment value to filter on.  

The Segment type and 
Segment valuedrop
-down lists appear only when 
the segments feature is enabled(on page 407 ) in your system.

#### Adjustment Box
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 197*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Adjustment Box 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details > 
Adjustment`


Use the Adjustment box to adjust working budgets in bulk for multiple properties and accounts. 

This page contains two sections: 

• 
The top section allows you to make percent adjustments to the working budget of one or 

more properties. You can apply the adjustment to all accounts or select specific account 

groups. 

• 
The bottom section, Adjustment history, displays a history log of budget adjustments, 

including the property to which it applies, the date and time that the adjustment was made, 

the user who made the adjustment, and the percentage by which the budget was adjusted. 

---



##### Adjusting a Working Budget in Bulk
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 198*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Adjusting a Working Budget in Bulk 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budgets > Budget Details > 
Adjustment`


You can adjust working budgets in bulk for multiple properties and accounts. 
To adjust at the account level, see Adjusting a Working Budget by Account(on page 184).

Adjusting a Working Budget in Bulk 

To make an adjustment for multiple properties: 

1. 
On the Working budget summary page, click the Adjustment button. 

The Adjustment box opens. 

2. 
In the Accounts field, select whether you want to apply the adjustment to all accounts or to 

one or more specific account groups: 

• 
All accounts: Select this option to adjust all accounts in the working budget. 

• 
Select account group: Select this option to adjust only the accounts included in the 

selected account group(s). If you select this option, then the Account group 

drop-down list appears for you to select the desired account group(s). 

3. 
In the Location drop-down list, select the property for which you are adjusting the budget. 

4. 
Select the itemization row(s) to which the adjustment should apply. 

5. 
In the Percent adjustment field, type the percentage by which you are adjusting the budget 

amounts. The Percent adjustment increases the budgeted amount for each budget period 

by the percentage specified; for example, if the budget for a period is $15,000, and you 

adjust it by 2.5% (expressed in the system as 2.50), then the budget will update to 

$15,375.00. 

6. 
Click the Adjust button. 

The Adjustment box closes and returns to the Working budget summary page. 

7. 
Filter the list as needed, and then click the Refresh button to update the data and 

recalculate the budget totals based on the adjustment(s). 

Viewing the Adjustment History of a Working Budget 

The system records a history log of all adjustments made to a working budget.  

To view the adjustment history: 

1. 
On the Working budget summary page, click the Adjustment button. 

The Adjustment box opens. 

2. 
Scroll down to the Adjustment history section. The log records the following information: 

---


<!-- Page 199 -->
Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

• 
Location: This is the property to which the adjustment applied. 

• 
Date: This is the date and time at which the adjustment was made. 

• 
Adjusted by: This is the user who made the adjustment. 

• 
Percent adjusted: The is the percentage by which the budget was adjusted. 
The Adjustment historylog does not include any adjustments made at the account level.

---

#### Payroll
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 210*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Payroll 

This section describes how to plan a payroll for a working budget. 

---



#### Payroll Planning Workflow
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 211*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Payroll Planning Workflow 

When planning a working budget, set up the following payroll definitions so the system can run 

the appropriate payroll calculations: 

1. 
Worker compensation classes: (on page 202) Define classifications to estimate 

compensation rates for employees based on the amount of risk involved in their job.  

2. 
Job positions: (on page 209) Add job positions for your employees, assign them to a worker 

compensation class, and define their pay type and pay frequency. 

3. 
Federal (on page 226) and state taxes (on page 233): Define all federal and state tax rates 

to use in your payroll calculations.  

4. 
Benefits: (on page 240) Define employee benefits such as health insurance and 401(k) plans.  

5. 
Employees: (on page 215) Add payroll records for each employee and designate their job 

position.  

After you set up these items, you can use the Payroll workbench (on page 248) to create and 

manage your company’s payroll(s) and publish their budgeted amounts to a working budget. 

---



#### Worker Compensation Class List
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 212*



##### Before You Begin

Worker Compensation Class List 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Worker Compensation Class`


Use the Worker compensation class list to create and manage class codes for worker 

compensation as part of your payroll’s working budget. Class codes identify specific categories of 

work so you can estimate workers’ compensation rates appropriate for the risk associated with 

the work being performed. 

From this list, you can: 

• 
Add, view, edit, or delete a worker compensation class. 

• 
Import a list of worker compensation classes using a mapping template. 

• 
Copy a worker compensation class from a previous year. 

• 
Export the list of worker compensation classes. 

Before You Begin 

Access can vary depending on your organization’s subscriptions and your permissions. If you do 

not see the option in the menu, then ask your System Administrator to check your permissions. 

Permissions 

These permissions are required to work with budget information for worker compensation 

classes. 

Application 
Activities/Lists 
Permissions 

Forecasting & Planning 
Worker compensation 

class 

List, View, Add, Edit, Delete 

If your user account doesn’t have the permissions shown above, then ask your System 

Administrator for assistance. If you have administrator privileges, you can assign permissions for a 

new feature to a user or to a role. See Roles and Permissions for a description of how to review a 

user’s permissions.

##### Adding a Worker Compensation Class

##### Viewing and Editing Worker Compensation Classes
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 213*

Adding a Worker Compensation Class 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > 
 Worker Compensation Class`


To add a worker compensation class: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the 
 Add icon next to Worker compensation class. 

The Worker compensation class page opens. 

3. 
In the Name field, type a unique name or code for the worker compensation class. 

4. 
Select a state from the State drop-down list. 

5. 
In the G/L account drop-down list, select a General Ledger account to map to the worker 

compensation class. 

6. 
In the Year field, type the year to associate with the class and compensation rate, formatted 

as YYYY. 

7. 
In the Rate field, type the compensation rate to associate with the class. 

8. 
Click Save. 

Viewing and Editing Worker Compensation Classes 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Worker Compensation Class > View or 
Edit`


To view or edit a worker compensation class: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, select the Worker compensation class menu option. 

The Worker compensation class list opens. 

3. 
Find the class that you want to view or change, and then click the View or Edit link. 

The Worker compensation class information page opens. 

When viewing a worker compensation class record, you can: 

• 
Click the Edit button to switch to Edit mode, and then make changes to the record as 

needed. 

• 
Click the Duplicate button to create a copy of the current worker compensation class 

record. 

• 
Click the Done button to close the record and return to the Worker compensation 

class list. 

• 
Click the More actions > View audit trail option to open the Audit trail box, which 

displays a log of who has changed the record and when they made the changes.

##### Copying Records from a Previous Year
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 215*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Copying Records from a Previous Year 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > (Payroll List) > Copy from Prior Year`


You can copy payroll records from a previous year if you want to reuse their details for the current 

or upcoming year. This functionality applies to the following payroll items: 

• 
Worker compensation classes 

• 
Federal taxes 

• 
State taxes 

• 
Benefits 

To use this option, details for the year you are copying from must already exist in the system. For 

example, if you are creating worker compensation classes for 2022 using the same details as the 

previous year, then records for the 2021 classes must exist in the Worker compensation class list. 
You can save time by copying all of the items from a previous year and then editing them rather than adding new records to the list individually.

To copy records from a previous year: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, under Payroll, select one of the following menu options: 

• 
Worker compensation class 

• 
Federal tax 

• 
State tax 

• 
Benefits 

The corresponding list opens. 

3. 
Click the Copy from prior year button. 

The Copy from prior year box opens. 

4. 
In the Select year drop-down list, select the year from which to copy details. 
The drop-down list populates only the years for which there are existing records.

5. 
In the Enter a year to copy to field, type the year to which you are copying the details. 

6. 
Click the Copy button. 

Any records linked to the previous year are copied into the list under the current or 

upcoming year.  

---

Job Positions List 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Job Positions`


Use the Job positions list to add and manage job positions and plan their payroll details for a 

working budget. Each job position is associated with a pay type (salary/hourly), pay frequency 

(bi-weekly, monthly, etc.), and worker compensation class (on page 202).  

From this list, you can: 

• 
Add a job position. 

• 
Import a list of job positions using a mapping template. 

• 
View or edit a job position. 

• 
Delete a job position. 

• 
Export the list of job positions. 

Before You Begin

not see the option in the menu, then ask your System Administrator to check your permissions.

Administrator for assistance. If you have administrator privileges, you can assign permissions for a 

new feature to a user or to a role. See Roles and Permissions for a description of how to review a

##### Adding a Job Position
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 220*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Adding a Job Position 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > 
 Job Positions`


To add a job position: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the 
 Add icon next to Job positions.  

The New job position page opens. 

3. 
In the Job position name field, type the name of the job position as it should appear in your 

company’s payroll. 

4. 
In the Worker compensation type drop-down list, select the worker compensation class 

appropriate for the job position. 
If no options appear in the drop-down list, or if the list does not include the worker 
compensation class needed for the job position, then you will need to create a new class in the Worker compensation classlist.

5. 
In the Pay frequency drop-down list, select the appropriate pay period for the job position: 

• 
Monthly: Employees in this job position are paid once a month on a set date and 

receive 12 paychecks per year. 

• 
Semi-monthly: Employees in this job position are paid twice a month and receive 24 

paychecks per year. For example, the payroll schedule may be set for the 15th and 

30th of each month. 

• 
Weekly: Employees in this job position are paid on the same day each week and 

receive 52 paychecks per year. This pay frequency is most common for hourly 

employees whose work hours may vary week-to-week. 

• 
Bi-weekly: Employees in this job position are paid every other week on the same day, 

such as every other Friday, and receive 26 paychecks per year. In some months, 

employees on a bi-weekly payroll receive three paychecks instead of two. In some 

years, employees receive a total of 27 paychecks. 

6. 
In the G/L account drop-down list, select an account from the General Ledger to associate 

with the job position. 

7. 
In the Type field, select whether the job position is salary or hourly pay: 

• 
Salary: Employees are paid a fixed, predetermined amount based on their annual 

salary. 

• 
Hourly: Employees are paid based on the specific number of hours they work. 

8. 
Click Save. 

---

#### Employees List

Employees List 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Employees`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Time & Resources > All > Employees`


Use the Employees list to add and manage employee records. Employee records are used when 

working on your payroll as part of a working budget in the Forecasting & Planning application, 

and for tracking time worked in the Time & Resources application. The system has two separate 

lists for creating employee records:  

• 
Employee records on the All tab of Forecasting & Planning are used for payroll, and for 

time-tracking in the Time & Resources application. 

• 
Employee records on the All tab of Company and the Setup tab of Forecasting & Planning, 

General Ledger, and Reports are dimensions that you can use in reports. 

This topic and its sub-topics pertain only to employee records used for payroll and time-tracking. 

From this list, you can: 

• 
Add, view, edit, or delete an employee record. 

• 
Click the link in the Login ID column to open the employee’s User record. 

• 
Export the list of employees. 

• 
Include inactive employees in the list.

annually. 

14. 
In the Annual increase (%) field, type the percentage by which the employee’s salary or 

hourly rate increases annually. 

15. 
In the 
 Annual effective date field, type or select the date that the employee’s Annual 

PTO hours, Annual sick time, and Annual increase (%) take effect in the payroll each year. 

16. 
In the External employee ID field, type the employee’s identification number. 

17. 
If you are allocating the employee’s time and expenses across multiple departments, select 

the Allocated employee check box. 

The following table appears: 

18. 
Complete the fields in the table for each department to which you are allocating the 

employee: 

• 
In the Entity drop-down list, select the entity. 

• 
In the Department drop-down list, select the department in which the employee 

works. 

• 
In the Allocation % field, type the percentage of time that the employee spends in the 

department. 

• 
In the Start date field, type the date that the employee started working in the 

department. 

• 
In the End date field, type the date that the employee stopped working in the 

department. Leave this field blank if the employee still works there. 

The Total percentage for the 
Allocation % column must equal 100%.

19. 
When finished, click Save to save the employee payroll record. 

---



##### Viewing and Editing Employee Records
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 229*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Viewing and Editing Employee Records 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Employees > View/Edit`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Time & Resources > All > Employees > View/Edit`


To view or edit an employee record on your payroll: 

1. 
Open the menu of the desired application, Forecasting & Planning or Time & Resources. 

2. 
Select the Employees menu option. The Employees list opens. 
Do not confuse this list with the Employeeslist on the 
Setup tab, which pertains to report 
dimensions.

3. 
Find the employee that you want to view or change, and then click the View or Edit link. 

The Employee page opens. 

When viewing an employee record, you can: 

• 
Click the Edit button to switch to Edit mode, and then make changes to the record as 

needed. 

• 
Click the Duplicate button to create a copy of the current employee record. 

• 
Click the Done button to close the record and return to the Employees list. 

• 
Click the More actions > View audit trail option to open the Audit trail box, which 

displays a log of who has changed the record and when they made the changes. 

When editing an employee record, you can: 

• 
Click the Duplicate button to create a copy of the current employee record. 

• 
Click the More actions > View audit trail option to open the Audit trail box, which 

displays a log of who has changed the record and when they made the changes. 

• 
Change or update fields on the Employee page. 

• 
Save or cancel the changes. 

---

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide

Deleting an Employee Record 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Employees > Delete`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Time & Resources > All > Employees > Delete`


To delete an employee record from the payroll: 

1. 
Open the menu of the desired application, Forecasting & Planning or Time & Resources. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, select the Employees option.  

The Employees list opens. 

3. 
Click the Delete link next to the desired employee. 

A confirmation box appears. 

4. 
Click OK to delete the record.

#### Employee Page
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 231*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Employee Page 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > 
 Employees`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Employees > View/Edit`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Time & Resources > All > 
 Employees`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Time & Resources > All > Employees > View/Edit`


Use the Employee page to add, view, or edit employee records as part of a working budget for 

your payroll and for recording time and resources. This page contains two sections: 

• 
(General information): Defines employment information about the employee, such as their 

name, job position, and salary details.  

• 
Allocated employees: Allows you to allocate an employee’s time and expenses across 

multiple departments. 
This page is not to be confused with the Employee page in the 
Setup tab, which pertains to 
report dimensions.

At a minimum, you must fill in the following fields to save a new employee record: 

• 
First name 

• 
Last name 

• 
Entity 

• 
Start date 

• 
Job position 

• 
State tax location 

• 
Salary 

If you are allocating an employee’s payroll—for example, if you have an employee who spends 

their work week in two separate departments—then the following fields in the Allocated employee 

section are also required: 

• 
Entity 

• 
Allocation %

#### Federal Taxes List
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 236*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Federal Taxes List 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Federal Tax`


When budgeting, it’s important to consider the following employee tax withholdings: 

• 
Federal income tax (based on withholding tables in Publication 15) 

• 
Social Security tax (up to the annual maximum, or cap) 

• 
Medicare tax 

• 
State income tax 

• 
Various local taxes (such as city, county, or school district taxes, state disability, or 

unemployment insurance) 

Use the Federal taxes list to add and manage federal payroll tax rates when creating and planning 

a working budget for your company. The system uses these rates to calculate the amount of 

federal income taxes that must be withheld from each employee’s paycheck. You can add a new 

set of tax rates to the list each year to maintain accuracy. 

From this list, you can: 

• 
Add federal taxes by year. 

• 
Import a list of federal taxes using a mapping template. 

• 
View or edit federal taxes. 

• 
Delete federal taxes. 

• 
Export the list of federal taxes. 

State payroll taxes are managed separately in the State taxeslist (on page 
233).

---



##### Before You Begin
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 237*



##### Adding Federal Taxes
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 237*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Before You Begin 

Access can vary depending on your organization’s subscriptions and your permissions. If you do 

not see the option in the menu, then ask your System Administrator to check your permissions. 

Permissions 

These permissions are required to work with budget information for federal taxes. 

Application 
Activities/Lists 
Permissions 

Forecasting & Planning 
Federal tax 
List, View, Add, Edit, Delete 

If your user account doesn’t have the permissions shown above, then ask your System 

Administrator for assistance. If you have administrator privileges, you can assign permissions for a 

new feature to a user or to a role. See Roles and Permissions for a description of how to review a 

user’s permissions. 

Adding Federal Taxes 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > 
 Federal Tax`


In RealPage Accounting, you can set up federal payroll taxes by year, and then the system will 

automatically update the budgeting calculations at the start of each year. 

To add a set of federal tax rates for a new year: 

1. 
In the Name field, type a name for the set of tax rates; for example, Federal Tax 2021. 

2. 
In the Year field, type the year when the tax rates should be applied to the budget, 

formatted as YYYY. 

3. 
In the G/L account drop-down list, select an account from the General Ledger to map to the 

federal taxes. 

4. 
In the remaining fields, type the tax rates and cap amounts: 

• 
FUTA %: This is the Federal Unemployment Tax rate. 

• 
FUTA Cap: This is the amount that is taxable from each employee’s wages or salary.  

• 
SSI%: This is the Supplemental Security Income (SSI) tax rate. 

• 
SSI Cap: This is the amount that is taxable from each employee’s wages or salary. 

• 
Medicare %: This is the Medicare tax rate.  

• 
Other Tax %: This is any other tax rate that is applied to each employee’s wages or 

salary. 

5. 
Click Save. 

---

3. 
Find the federal taxes that you want to view or change, and then click the View or Edit link. 

The Federal tax page opens. 

When viewing a federal tax record, you can: 

• 
Click the Edit button to switch to Edit mode, and then make changes to the record as 

needed. 

• 
Click the Duplicate button to create a copy of the current record. 

• 
Click the Done button to close the record and return to the Federal taxes list. 

• 
Click the More actions > View audit trail option to open the Audit trail box, which 

displays a log of who has changed the record and when they made the changes. 

When editing an employee record, you can: 

• 
Click the Duplicate button to create a copy of the current record. 

• 
Click the More actions > View audit trail option to open the Audit trail box, which 

displays a log of who has changed the record and when they made the changes. 

• 
Change or update fields on the Federal tax page. 

• 
Save or cancel the changes. 

Deleting Federal Taxes 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Federal Tax > Delete`


To delete a federal payroll tax: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, select the Federal tax option.  

The Federal taxes list opens. 

3. 
Click the Delete link next to the desired federal tax record. 

A confirmation box appears. 

4. 
Click OK to delete the record.

##### Copying Records from a Previous Year
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 239*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Copying Records from a Previous Year 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > (Payroll List) > Copy from Prior Year`


You can copy payroll records from a previous year if you want to reuse their details for the current 

or upcoming year. This functionality applies to the following payroll items: 

• 
Worker compensation classes 

• 
Federal taxes 

• 
State taxes 

• 
Benefits 

To use this option, details for the year you are copying from must already exist in the system. For 

example, if you are creating worker compensation classes for 2022 using the same details as the 

previous year, then records for the 2021 classes must exist in the Worker compensation class list. 
You can save time by copying all of the items from a previous year and then editing them rather than adding new records to the list individually.

To copy records from a previous year: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, under Payroll, select one of the following menu options: 

• 
Worker compensation class 

• 
Federal tax 

• 
State tax 

• 
Benefits 

The corresponding list opens. 

3. 
Click the Copy from prior year button. 

The Copy from prior year box opens. 

4. 
In the Select year drop-down list, select the year from which to copy details. 

The drop-down list populates only the years for which there are existing records.

5. 
In the Enter a year to copy to field, type the year to which you are copying the details. 

6. 
Click the Copy button. 

Any records linked to the previous year are copied into the list under the current or 

upcoming year.  

---

State Taxes List 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > State Tax`


When budgeting, it’s important to consider the following employee tax withholdings: 

• 
Federal income tax (based on withholding tables in Publication 15) 

• 
Social Security tax (up to the annual maximum, or cap) 

• 
Medicare tax 

• 
State income tax 

• 
Various local taxes (such as city, county, or school district taxes, state disability, or 

unemployment insurance) 

As each state differs, so do their tax rates and types of taxes. Use the State taxes list to add and 

manage state payroll tax rates when creating and planning a working budget for your company. 

The system uses these rates to calculate the amount of state income taxes that must be withheld 

from each employee’s paycheck. You can add a new set of tax rates to the list each year to 

maintain accuracy for each state in your budget. 

From this list, you can: 

• 
Add state taxes by state and year. 

• 
Import a list of state taxes using a mapping template. 

• 
View or edit state taxes. 

• 
Delete state taxes. 

• 
Export the list of state taxes. 

Federal payroll taxes, including Social Security and Medicare taxes, are maintained separately in the Federal taxeslist (on page 
226).

#### Benefits List
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 250*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Benefits List 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Benefits`


Just as each company has differences in the way they pay their employees, they all offer their 

employees a different benefits package. Use the Benefits list to add and manage the benefits that 

your company offers and define different rates for different states to maintain accuracy in your 

payroll budget. Benefits include costs such as health insurance, 401(k) plans, gym subsidies, and 

more. 

From this list, you can: 

• 
Add benefits by state and year. 

• 
Import a list of benefits using a mapping template. 

• 
View or edit benefits. 

• 
Delete benefits. 

• 
Export the full list of benefits. 

---



##### Adding a Benefit
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 251*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Adding a Benefit 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > 
 Benefits`


If you have a large number of benefit options throughout multiple states, consider importing the 

benefits to save time in the setup process. You can also copy existing benefits from a previous 

year (on page 204) to streamline this process. 

To add a new benefit manually: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the 
 Add icon next to Benefits.  

The Benefit page opens. 

3. 
In the Name field, type a name for the benefit as you want it to appear in the Benefits list. 

4. 
In the State drop-down list, select the state to which the payroll benefit applies. 

5. 
In the Type field, select a calculation method for the benefit: 

• 
Fixed: This runs the benefit figures as fixed dollar amounts when calculating payroll. 

• 
Percentage: This runs the benefit figures as a percentage of the employee’s total 

compensation when calculating payroll. 

6. 
In the Average amount field, type the average dollar amount or percent for each month. 

7. 
Click the Calculate button. 

The average amount or percent automatically populates in the Periods section for each 

month. You can adjust the dollar amount or percent of each month, as necessary. The 

system automatically calculates the Total dollar amount or percent for the year. 

8. 
In the Year field, type the year to which the benefit amounts apply. 

9. 
In the G/L account drop-down list, select the General Ledger account to map to the benefit 

figures. 

10. 
Click Save. 

---
