Viewing and Editing Benefits 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Benefits > View or Edit`


To view or edit benefits: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, select the Benefits menu option. 

The Benefits list opens. 

3. 
Find the payroll benefit that you want to view or change, and then click the View or Edit 

link. 

The Benefit page opens. 

When viewing a state tax record, you can: 

• 
Click the Edit button to switch to Edit mode, and then make changes to the record as 

needed. 

• 
Click the Duplicate button to create a copy of the current record. 

• 
Click the Done button to close the record and return to the Benefits list. 

• 
Click the More actions > View audit trail option to open the Audit trail box, which 

displays a log of who has changed the record and when they made the changes. 

When editing an employee record, you can: 

• 
Click the Duplicate button to create a copy of the current record. 

• 
Click the More actions > View audit trail option to open the Audit trail box, which 

displays a log of who has changed the record and when they made the changes. 

• 
Change or update fields on the Benefit page. 

• 
Save or cancel the changes. 

Deleting a Benefit 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Benefits > Delete`


To delete a benefit: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, select the Benefits option.  

The Benefits list opens. 

3. 
Click the Delete link next to the desired payroll benefit. 

A confirmation box appears. 

4. 
Click OK to delete the record.

##### Copying Records from a Previous Year
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 253*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Copying Records from a Previous Year 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > (Payroll List) > Copy from Prior Year`


You can copy payroll records from a previous year if you want to reuse their details for the current 

or upcoming year. This functionality applies to the following payroll items: 

• 
Worker compensation classes 

• 
Federal taxes 

• 
State taxes 

• 
Benefits 

To use this option, details for the year you are copying from must already exist in the system. For 

example, if you are creating worker compensation classes for 2022 using the same details as the 

previous year, then records for the 2021 classes must exist in the Worker compensation class list. 
You can save time by copying all of the items from a previous year and then editing them rather than adding new records to the list individually.

To copy records from a previous year: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, under Payroll, select one of the following menu options: 

• 
Worker compensation class 

• 
Federal tax 

• 
State tax 

• 
Benefits 

The corresponding list opens. 

3. 
Click the Copy from prior year button. 

The Copy from prior year box opens. 

4. 
In the Select year drop-down list, select the year from which to copy details. 
The drop-down list populates only the
years for which there are existing records.

5. 
In the Enter a year to copy to field, type the year to which you are copying the details. 

6. 
Click the Copy button. 

Any records linked to the previous year are copied into the list under the current or 

upcoming year.  

---

Before You Begin

#### Payroll List
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 259*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Payroll List 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Payroll Workbench`


When you select the Payroll workbench option in the Forecasting & Planning menu, the Payroll 

list opens with a list of payrolls created for your company. You can use this list to calculate new 

payrolls and publish them to a working budget (on page 158), which displays a summary of G/L 

account details for each payroll item—such as workers’ compensation, benefits, and federal and 

state taxes—by budget period.  

In addition, you can use the list to: 

• 
View the employees included in a payroll. 

• 
View the publish history of a payroll. 

• 
Refresh a payroll to update its calculations. 

• 
Delete a payroll. 

• 
Export the full list of payrolls. 

---



##### Adding and Calculating a Payroll
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 260*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Adding and Calculating a Payroll 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > 
 Payroll Workbench`


When you add a new payroll to the Payroll list, the system automatically begins processing the 

payroll calculations for each employee on the payroll. 

To add a payroll: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the 
 Add icon next to Payroll workbench.  

The Payroll workbench page opens. 

3. 
In the Working budget drop-down list, select the working budget to which you are 

publishing your payroll details. 

The Year field populates with the year assigned to the working budget. 

4. 
In the Entity/location drop-down list, select the entity or location to which the payroll 

applies. Only the entities or locations associated with the working budget appear in the drop
- down 
list.

5. 
In the Employee drop-down list, select one or more employees to include in the payroll. 
Only the employees(on page 215 ) associated with the selected entity or location appear 
in the drop-down list. 

6. 
Click Calculate. 

The payroll is added to the Payrolls list and the system automatically begins processing the 

payroll calculations for each employee. The Status column will change from “Calculating” to 

“Calculated” when the system finishes processing the payroll. Only then can you publish (on 

page 250) the payroll to the working budget. This process may take a few minutes.  

---



##### Publishing a Payroll to a Working Budget
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 261*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Publishing a Payroll to a Working Budget 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Payroll Workbench > Publish`


After you add a new payroll to the workbench and the system calculates the payroll for each 

employee, you can publish the payroll details to your working budget. 

To publish a payroll to a working budget: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the Payroll workbench option.  

The Payroll list opens. 

3. 
Find the desired payroll, and then click the Publish link. 
This link is not available if the Status of the payroll is still 
“Calculating.
”

A confirmation box opens. 

4. 
Click OK to continue. 

The Status of the payroll changes from “Calculated” to “Publishing.” When the payroll 

finishes publishing to the working budget, the Status will change to “Published.” 
You can republish a payroll at any time to update the budget details; for example, if you have added a new employee to the payroll.

---



##### Viewing the Publish History of a Payroll
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 262*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Viewing Payroll Details in the Working Budget 

When the payroll finishes publishing, go to the Working budget summary page (on page 163) to 

view the G/L account details for each payroll item. 

Viewing the Publish History of a Payroll 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Payroll Workbench > (Status)`


After a payroll is published to a working budget, the system records the publish details in a 

history log, including when the payroll was published and who published it. Each time that you 

republish the payroll, a new entry appears in the history log. 

To view the publish history of a payroll: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the Payroll workbench option.  

The Payroll list opens. 

3. 
Find the desired payroll, and then select the Published link (or Publishing link if publishing 

is underway) in the Status column. 

---



##### Refreshing a Payroll’s Calculations
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 263*



##### Deleting a Payroll
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 263*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

The Publish payroll history box opens with a list of all previous publish details. 

Refreshing a Payroll’s Calculations 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Payroll Workbench > Refresh`


If you have added an employee to the payroll, modified an employee payroll record, or modified 

payroll items such as benefits, federal and state taxes, or workers’ compensation, you can refresh 

the payroll to recalibrate its calculations and maintain an accurate budget. 
You will also need to republish the payroll to the working budget after you update the calculations.

To refresh a payroll’s calculations: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the Payroll workbench option.  

The Payroll list opens. 

3. 
Find the desired payroll, and then click the Refresh link.  

The system recalculates the payroll; this process may take a few minutes. 

The Refresh link is not available if the 
Status of the pa
yroll is still “Calculating.
”

Deleting a Payroll 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Payroll Workbench > Delete`


To delete a payroll from the workbench: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, select the Payroll workbench option. The Payroll list opens. 

3. 
Click the Delete link next to the desired payroll. A confirmation box appears. 

4. 
Click OK to delete the record. 

---

Fields 

Payroll List

name 

This is the name of the entity or location for which the payroll was created.

This is the working budget to which the payroll calculations apply. 

Status 
This is the status of the payroll: 

• 
Calculating: The system is processing the payroll calculations for 

each employee on the payroll based on the settings defined for 

various payroll items (workers’ compensation, federal and state 

taxes, benefits, etc.). 

• 
Calculated: The system is finished processing the payroll calculations 

and the payroll is ready to be published to the working budget. 

• 
Publishing: The system is publishing the payroll to the working 

budget. 

• 
Published: The system is finished publishing the payroll to the 

working budget. You can now access the budget details to view a 

summary of the payroll accounts and their budget figures. 

Modified by 
This is the user who created the payroll and/or last published the payroll.

system records the publish details in the Publish payroll history box (on 

page 250). 

Refresh (link) 
Click this link to refresh the payroll calculations; for example, if you have 

updated payroll details such as worker compensation rates, tax rates, 

benefits, etc.

*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 267*

Employees of (Entity Name) List 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Payroll Workbench > (Entity/Location ID)`


You can view a list of all employees added to a particular payroll by drilling down into the 

Entity/location ID column from the Payroll list. Clicking this link opens the Employees of (entity 

name) list, which displays a summary of each employee’s payroll details. 

From this list, you can: 

• 
Add employees to the payroll. 

• 
View the payroll calculations of an individual employee. 

• 
Export the full list of employees in the payroll. 

Adding an Employee to the Payroll 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Payroll Workbench > (Entity/Location ID) 
> Add Employee`


To add an employee to a payroll: 

1. 
In the Payroll list, find the desired payroll, and then click the link in the Entity/location ID 

column. 

The Employees of (entity name) list opens. 

2. 
Click the Add employee button. 

The Payroll workbench box opens. 

3. 
In the Employee drop-down list, select the employee(s) you want to add to the payroll. 

4. 
Click the Save button. 
Make sure you refresh the payroll(on page 253) in the Payroll list to run the payroll 
calculations for the new employee(s), and then republish the payroll(on page 250) to update the working budget.

##### Buttons

#### Management Fees List
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 271*



##### Calculating Management Fees
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 271*

Chapter 4: Periodic Tasks 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Management Fees List 

Use the Management fees list to calculate and publish management fees to a working budget. 

The information for each management fee is based on the fee template that has been set up in 

the Accounts Payable application.  

Calculating Management Fees 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Management Fees > Calculate`


You can calculate a management fee to retrieve the budget amounts for the billed entity for the 

G/L account on the fee. 

To calculate management fees: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the Management fees option.  

The Management fees list opens. 

3. 
Find the desired fee, and then click the Calculate link. 

The Management fees box opens. 

4. 
In the Year drop-down list, select the year for which you are calculating. 

The budget amounts for the Billed entity populate in the table below. 

5. 
Click the Calculate button. 

The system processes the calculations; you can now publish the management fee (on page 

261) to a working budget. 

---



##### Publishing Management Fees to a Working Budget
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 272*

Periodic Tasks 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Publishing Management Fees to a Working Budget 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Management Fees > Publish`


After you calculate the management fee (on page 261), you can publish the budget amounts for 

the billed entity to a working budget. 

To publish a management fee: 

1. 
Open the Forecasting & Planning application’s All tab. 

2. 
In the Periodic tasks section, click the Management fees option.  

The Management fees list opens. 

3. 
Find the desired fee, and then click the Publish link. 

The Management fees box opens. 

4. 
In the Working budget drop-down list, select the working budget to which you are 

publishing. 
Only working budgets for the applicable year appear in the drop-down list. This is the year 
that you selected when calculating the management fee.

5. 
Click the Publish button. 

The Publish history section at the bottom of the Management fees box maintains a log of 

the management fee’s publish history, including which working budget it was published to, 

which property the fee was budgeted for, when it was published, and who published it. 

---

#### My Stored Reports List
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 278*

Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

My Stored Reports List 

See the How to Get Here (on page 269) topic for a list of ways to access this list. 

Reports are added to the My stored reports list when you process them offline or schedule them 

to run at a later date. 

• 
Processing and storing reports: You have the option to process a report in the background 

and store it in the system. This option is particularly useful for reports containing amounts 

of data that are too large for the system to run in real time. You can also use this feature to 

take and store periodic snapshots automatically for future reference. 

• 
Scheduling reports: You have the option to store reports and set them up on a schedule to 

run periodically. After the system runs a scheduled report on the date you selected, the 

report is available in the menu of the application that you ran it from. 

About the My Stored Reports List 

The My stored reports list is available in any application that has reports.  

• 
When you access the list from an individual application, it will display only the reports that 

you scheduled or processed in that application. For example, if you open the My stored 

reports list in Accounts Payable, then you will see only Accounts Payable reports. 

• 
When you access the list from the Reports application, it will be centralized—that is, you will 

see all of your stored reports regardless of which application it came from. 

From the My stored reports list, you can: 

• 
Sort and filter the list of reports. 

• 
Save reports as a PDF file. 

• 
Open a report’s data in Excel. 

• 
Download the report data as a CSV file.  

• 
Export the list of stored reports. 

• 
View an error log for failed reports. 

About Stored Reports 

Note the following behavior of stored reports: 

• 
The data in stored reports does not reflect any changes beyond the report’s As of date. 

However, if you click any links in the report that open other pages, you will be accessing the 

latest data on those pages. 

• 
By default, the location ID and location name are appended to the end of the report name 

to easily identify the property for which the report was run. If you ran the report on a 

location group and did not choose to run it as an individual report, then the location group 

ID and location group name appears at the end of the report name instead. 

---

#### Storing Standard Reports and Charts
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 281*



##### Viewing a Stored Report
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 281*

Chapter 5: Reports 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Storing Standard Reports and Charts 

You can process any report or chart on demand and have the system store it. The Process & store 

option is particularly useful when running large reports. It is also useful to maintain reporting 

history for a specific point in time. 

After setting up a report or chart, you have these options: 

• 
To run the report or chart and view it, click the View button. 

• 
To run the report or chart and store it, click the Process & store button. 
You can process and store the report or chart either before or after viewing it.

Viewing a Stored Report 

After the system runs the report on the date that you selected, you can find the report in either 

the My stored reports list or the Reports center. 

To view a stored report in the My stored reports list: 

1. 
Open the application where you created the report. 

2. 
On the All tab of the menu, find and select the My stored reports option. 

The My stored reports list opens with a list of reports stored in the application. 

3. 
Find the report that you want to view and then verify the report date in the Run date/time 

column. Verifying the report date helps you avoid missing data. For example, if you 

scheduled the report to run two weeks ago, you would be missing the last two weeks of 

data. 

4. 
Click one of the following links to open your report in the format it was stored:  

• 
HTML: Opens the report in a new browser tab as a web page. 

• 
PDF: Opens a printable PDF version of the report that you can print and download. 

• 
CSV: Downloads a CSV file containing the report data. This is a good format to 

download if you want to import the data into another application. 

• 
Excel: Downloads the report as an Excel file. 

• 
Text: Opens a text version of the report in the web browser. 

• 
Cloud: Opens the report in your configured cloud account. If there is no cloud link for 

the report, see Storing a Report in a Cloud Storage Location (on page 277). 

• 
Success: Opens the report in Reporting Portal. If there was an error processing the 

report, then the View status link appears in the column instead. See Viewing a Report 

Error Log for error details. 
If you have not yet processed the report offline, then click the Create offline link to process 
the report.

---

Storing a Report in My Stored Reports 

You can generate reports on demand and store them in the My stored reports list for later 

access. 

To store a report in the My stored reports list: 

1. 
Locate the report, and then select the option to process or run it. 

• 
Financial reports: Click the Process & store link. 

• 
Report groups: Click the Run link. 

• 
Other reports: Display the report, and then click the Process & store button. 

The Storage options page opens. 

2. 
In the Schedule section, type or select the date on which to run the report in the Run report 

on field. Additional fields may appear if you have set up financial reports to prompt for filters at runtime. See Prompting for 
Filters at Runtime.If you are running a report group, a different set of fields will appear. See Running a Report Group on Demand.

3. 
In the Delivery options section, select the Store report option. 

4. 
In the Report name field, type a name for the report. 

If yo u ran the report on a location group and did not choose to run it as an individual report, then the 
Location IDand 
Nameis added to the end of the stored report name to easily 
identify which property the report is for.

5. 
In the Report type drop-down list, select a report format: 

• 
HTML: Saves a report as an HTML file. 

• 
Printable: Saves the report as a printable PDF file. 

• 
CSV: Saves the report in a comma-separated values (CSV) file. 

• 
Excel: Saves reports in an Excel file. 

• 
Text: Saves the report as a text file. 

On ly the 
Printable and 
Excel options are available for report groups. When storing report 
groups as an Excel file, each report appears on a separate tab.

6. 
Click OK.

##### Storing a Report in a Cloud-Storage Target
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 287*

Chapter 5: Reports 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Storing a Report in a Cloud-Storage Target 

You can deliver a report to a secure, cloud-based location such as Dropbox, Box, Google Drive, or 

Amazon S3. If you are a developer, you can also deliver the report to another application using 

HTTP. 

To do this, you need to set up cloud-storage targets in RealPage Accounting. The targets that you 

define will be available as storage options for reports. 

To store a report in a cloud-storage target: 

1. 
Locate the report, and then select the option to process or run it. 

• 
Financial reports: Click the Process & store link. 

• 
Report groups: Click the Run link. 

• 
Other reports: Display the report, and then click the Process & store button. 

The Storage options page opens. 

2. 
In the Schedule section, type or select the date on which to run the report in the Run report 

on field. Additional fields may appear if you have set up financial reports to prompt for filters at runtime. See Prompting for Filters at Runtime.If you are running a report group, a different set of fields will appear. See Running a Report Group on Demand.

3. 
Select the Store in cloud check box. 

4. 
In the Send to drop-down list, select the cloud-storage target where you want to deliver the 

report.  By default, you can choose from cloud
-storage targets that either you
’ve defined or that 
others have defined and marked as public. If you’re an administrator, you can see all cloud 
targets.

5. 
If you want to override the default path where the report is delivered in the cloud-storage 

target, then type a different path in the Path field. 

6. 
In the Report name field, type a name for the report. 
If you ran the report on a location group and did not choose to run it as an individual report, then the Location IDand 
Nameis added to the end of the stored report name to easily 
identify which property the report is for.

7. 
In the Report type drop-down list, select a report format: 

• 
HTML: Saves a report as an HTML file. 

• 
Printable: Saves the report as a printable PDF file. 

---

This section appears only if you are running a report group. If you set any default parameters for 

the report group, then the system automatically populates those fields. You can override the 

default parameters as needed.

group. If the reports in the group are already filtered, then the filter you 

choose will be applied after the filters in the report’s definition are 

applied.

#### Memorized Reports
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 294*



##### Before You Begin
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 294*

Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Memorized Reports 

See the How to Get Here (on page 284) topic for a list of ways to access memorized reports. 

Memorizing a report retains the report parameters so you don’t have to select them each time 

you want to run or display the report. When you memorize a report, you can assign it a unique 

name so you can add the report to dashboards or report groups or schedule it to run on a 

recurring basis. We recommend that you test run the report with the desired parameters to verify 

that the report output is correct before memorizing the report. 
A memorized report can be public or private. Public reports are available to all system users. Private reports are available only to the report owner and system administrators.

Memorized Reports Locations 

Memorized reports appear in the Memorized reports list (on page 286) and in the Reports 

center. 

The Memorized reports list is accessible from most RealPage Financial Suite applications; 

however, it displays only the reports that were memorized in the application you access it from. 

For example, if you click the Memorized reports option in the Cash Management menu, then the 

list will include only reports memorized in Cash Management. 

Before You Begin 

Access can vary depending on your organization’s subscriptions and your permissions. If you do 

not see the option in the menu, then ask your System Administrator to check your permissions. 

Memorizing a report requires that you have the permissions to do so. You must have permissions 

to access and run a report in order to memorize it. Permissions for users, groups, or roles are 

configured in the Company setup. 

If your user account doesn’t have the permissions shown above, then ask your System 

Administrator for assistance. If you have administrator privileges, you can assign permissions for a 

new feature to a user or to a role. See Roles and Permissions for a description of how to review a 

user’s permissions. 

---



##### How to Get Here
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 295*



##### Memorizing a Report or Chart
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 295*

Chapter 5: Reports 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

How to Get Here 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Reports > Reports Center > Memorized`


1. 
Open the Reports menu. 

2. 
Select Reports Center. 

3. 
Select the Memorized tab. 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > (Application) > All > Reports > Memorized Reports`


1. 
Open the desired application. 

2. 
In the Reports column, select Memorized reports.  


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Property Management/Condo and Co-op > All > Reports > Custom 
Reports > Memorized Reports`


1. 
Open the desired application. 

2. 
In the Reports column, find the Custom Reports section, and then select Memorized reports. 

Memorizing a Report or Chart 

To memorize a report or chart: 

1. 
Set up a report or chart with the filters and parameters that you want to save. 

2. 
Click the Memorize button. The Memorize report parameters box opens. 

3. 
In the Name field, type a name for the report or chart. 
If you plan to make the report or chart public, give it a name that is easily identifiable to others.

4. 
Select the Public check box to make the report or chart available to other users in their 

Memorized reports list. Clear the check box to keep it private. Private reports and charts are 

visible only to the person who created them (owners), and administrators. 

5. 
Click OK. 

---



#### Memorized Reports List
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 296*

Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Memorized Reports List 

See the How to Get Here (on page 284) topic for a list of ways to access memorized reports. 

Use the Memorized reports list to view and manage reports that you saved in an application or 

that other users saved and then made public. From this list, you can: 

• 
Run memorized reports. 

• 
Edit memorized reports that you created. 

• 
Process large reports in the background and store them in the My stored reports list. 

• 
Schedule reports so that they run at a future date. 

• 
Delete memorized reports. 

• 
Export the list of memorized reports. 

Memorized reports are also accessible from the Reports center.

---



##### Editing a Memorized Report
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 297*

Chapter 5: Reports 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Editing a Memorized Report 

See the How to Get Here (on page 284) topic for a list of ways to access memorized reports. 

You can edit a memorized report or chart only if you are the owner or a system administrator, 

even if the report or chart was made public. As the owner, you can change any parameter, 

including who owns it. The only field you cannot change is the report type.  

To edit a memorized report or chart: 

1. 
In the Memorized reports list, click the Edit link next to the report or chart that you want to 

change. The Edit memorized report page opens. The fields on this page correspond with 

the report’s type.  

2. 
Update the fields in the General information section as needed: 

• 
In the Report name and Report description fields, type a new name and description 

for the report, respectively. 

• 
In the Report owner drop-down list, select a different user to be the owner of the 

report. If you change the report’s owner, then you will no longer be able to edit this 

memorized report after you save the changes. However, you can continue to run the 

report if it’s still public.  

• 
Select the Public check box to make the report available to other users in their 

Memorized reports list. Clear the check box to make the report private; that is, 

available only to yourself and system administrators. 
Others can see private memorized reports by selecting the Include privatecheck box 
in their Memorized reports list.

3. 
Modify the other settings as needed. 

4. 
Click Save. 

---



##### Running a Memorized Report or Chart
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 298*

Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Running a Memorized Report or Chart 

See the How to Get Here (on page 284) topic for a list of ways to access memorized reports. 

To run a memorized report or chart: 

1. 
In the Memorized reports list, find the report or chart you want to view, and then click the 

Run link.  

2. 
In the Select report type drop-down list, select a format for the report or chart output:  

• 
HTML: Saves the report as an HTML file so you can view it in your web browser. 

• 
Printable: Saves the report as a printable PDF file. 

• 
CSV: Saves the report as a comma-separated value (CSV) file so you can import the 

data into another application. 

• 
Excel: Saves the report as an Excel file so you can manipulate the data further. 

• 
Text: Saves the report as a basic text file.  
Charts are only available in Printable and 
HTML format.

3. 
Click OK to run the report. 

• 
If you selected Printable, HTML, or Text, then the report opens in a new window. 

• 
If you selected CSV or Excel, then the system saves the report in your local Downloads 

folder. 
You can also run a memorized report from the Reports center.

---



##### Scheduling a Memorized Report or Chart
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 299*

Chapter 5: Reports 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Scheduling a Memorized Report or Chart 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Reports > All > Reports Center > Memorized > More Actions > Edit 
Schedule`


See the How to Get Here (on page 284) topic for a list of ways to access memorized reports. 

Use the Schedule memorized report page to schedule a memorized report or chart to run 

automatically and routinely. 

Accessing the Schedule Memorized Report Page  

You can schedule a report or chart from either the Reports application or the application where it 

was originally memorized. 

To schedule a report from the Reports application: 

1. 
Open the Reports menu, and then click the Reports center option. The Reports center page 

opens.  

2. 
Select the Memorized tab. 

3. 
In the (View) drop-down list, select the application associated with the report, or select All 

applications. 

4. 
Find the report or chart you want to schedule, and then click More actions. 

5. 
Select the Edit schedule option. The Schedule memorized report (on page 295) page 

opens. 

To schedule a report from another application: 

1. 
In the Applications menu, select the application where the memorized report is located, and 

then select the All tab. 

2. 
In the Reports section, click the Memorized reports option. 

3. 
Find the report or chart you want to schedule, and then click the Schedule link. The 

Schedule memorized report (on page 295) page opens. 

Filtering the Report 

1. 
In the Department/group and Location/group drop-down lists, select a specific department 

or location (or group of departments or locations) by which to filter the report. If you leave 

the filters blank, then the report will run for all departments and locations. 

2. 
If you are scheduling a financial report and the report is set to prompt for G/L account 

groups, then the G/L account group selection box appears. Select one or more G/L account 

groups to filter the report by. 

---


<!-- Page 300 -->
Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

3. 
In the As of date field, type or select the date from which the scheduled report should 

display data. Leave this field blank to use the current date. If you enter an As of date, that 

date will always be used regardless of when the schedule is run. 
Type “ p ”in a date field to automatically insert the last day of the prior month. See 
Specifying Dates in Getting Started for a full list of shortcut keys related to entering dates.

If you enabled reporting account labels in the General Ledger, then you have the option to 

substitute the account labels in the report with the reporting account set’s labels: 

• 
To enable reporting account labels, select the Substitute with reporting accounts 

labels check box. The Reporting account set drop-down list appears. 

• 
In the Reporting account set drop-down list, select the reporting account set whose 

labels you want to use. 

Scheduling the Report 

1. 
In the Start date field, type or select the date on which to start running the report.  
Typically, the Start dateis a date in the future. If you select a date in the past, then the 
report executes immediately after you save it. The Start date also determines the date 
that the system schedules the report to run in future occurrences; for example, if you are scheduling the report to run weekly and the 
Start dateis on a Monday, then the system will 
run the report every Monday.Type “ p ”in a date field to automatically insert 
the last day of the prior month. See Specifying Dates in Getting Started for a full list of shortcut keys related to entering dates.

2. 
In the Ending on field, select when to stop running the report:  

• 
Never: Run the report indefinitely. 

• 
Number of occurrences: Stop running the report after a specific number of 

occurrences.  

• 
In the End after field, type the number of occurrences after which to stop running 

the report; for example, type “5” to stop after five occurrences. 

• 
End date: Stop running the report on a specific date.  

• 
In the End on field, type or select the date on which to stop running the report. 

3. 
In the Recurrence drop-down list, select an option: 

• 
None: Run the report once on the scheduled date. 

• 
Days: Run the report once every X days. 

• 
Weeks: Run the report once every X weeks. 

• 
Months: Run the report once every X months.  

---


<!-- Page 301 -->
Chapter 5: Reports 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

• 
Select the End of month check box to disregard the start date and run the report 

on the last day of the month. 

• 
Years: Run the report once a year. 

4. 
If you selected Days, Weeks, Months, or Years, type the number of times to run the report 

in the Every field.  

5. 
In the Email results to field, type the email address where you want the system to send the 

report results. 

• 
Click Save. 

Storing and Delivering the Report 

1. 
Select one or more delivery options for the report: 

• 
Email: Sends the report in an email. 

• 
Store report: Stores the report in the My stored reports list. 

• 
Store in cloud: Stores the report in a cloud-storage target. 

• 
Store in Reporting Portal: Stores the report in the selected owner portal(s). 

2. 
Fill in the fields corresponding with each section. 

Email 

1. 
In the To field, type the email address to which you want to send the report. 

2. 
Select the CC me check box if you want the system to send a copy of the email message to 

your inbox. 

3. 
In the Subject field, type the subject of the email message. 

4. 
In the Report type drop-down list, select a report format: 

• 
HTML: Sends the report as an HTML file. 

• 
Printable: Sends the report as a printable PDF file. 

• 
CSV: Sends the report in a comma-separated values (CSV) file. 

• 
Excel: Sends the report in an Excel file. 

• 
Text: Sends the report in a text file. 

5. 
In the Message field, type a message if you want to include one in the email message that 

includes the report. 

Store Report 

1. 
In the Report description field, type a description of the report to help you remember its 

purpose and content and to distinguish it from other stored reports. The description 

appears in the My stored reports list in the Title column. 

2. 
In the Report type drop-down list, select a report format: 

---


<!-- Page 302 -->
Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

• 
HTML: Saves the report as an HTML file. 

• 
Printable: Saves the report as a printable PDF file. 

• 
CSV: Saves the report in a comma-separated values (CSV) file. 

• 
Excel: Saves the report in an Excel file. 

• 
Text: Saves the report in a text file. 

Store in Cloud 

1. 
In the Send to drop-down list, select a cloud-storage target where you want to deliver the 

report. By default, you can choose from any cloud-storage targets that you’ve defined or 

that others have defined and marked as public. If you’re an administrator, you can see all 

cloud targets. 

2. 
Optionally, you can use the Path field to override the default location where the report will 

be delivered. 

3. 
In the Report name field, type the title that should be used for the report. 

4. 
In the Report type drop-down list, select a report format: 

• 
HTML: Saves the report as an HTML file. 

• 
Printable: Saves the report as a printable PDF file. 

• 
CSV: Saves the report in a comma-separated values (CSV) file. 

• 
Excel: Saves the report in an Excel file. 

• 
Text: Saves the report in a simple text file. 

Store in Reporting Portal 

1. 
In the Report name field, type the name of the report as you want it to appear in the owner 

portal(s). 

2. 
In the Report type drop-down list, select a report format: 

• 
HTML: Saves the report as an HTML file. 

• 
Printable: Saves the report as a printable PDF file. 

• 
CSV: Saves the report in a comma-separated values (CSV) file. 

• 
Excel: Saves the report in an Excel file. 

3. 
In the Publish to field, select the folder to which you want to upload the report. 
The system creates default folders based on the reporting periods set up in General Ledger; however, you can create custom folders to upload documents to on the Configurationpage for the
 Reporting Portalapplication.

4. 
Select the Owner(s) for which you are uploading the report. 

Saving the Report Schedule 

When you have finished configuring the report schedule and delivery options, click Save to 

schedule the memorized report.

##### Deleting a Memorized Report or Chart
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 303*

Chapter 5: Reports 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Deleting a Memorized Report or Chart 

See the How to Get Here (on page 284) topic for a list of ways to access memorized reports and 

charts. 

Deleting a memorized report or chart removes it from the Memorized reports list and also 

removes the report or chart from any dashboard on which it appears.  
You must be either the report or chart owner or an administrator to delete it.

To delete a memorized report: 

1. 
Open the Memorized reports list. 

2. 
Find the report or chart that you want to delete, and then click the Delete link. A 

confirmation box opens. 

3. 
Click OK.  

---

Reports

Edit Memorized Report Page 

See the How to Get Here (on page 284) topic for a list of ways to access memorized reports. 

Use the Edit memorized report page to change reports and charts (graphs). You can change 

general information about the memorized report or chart, and change the parameters that are 

memorized. 

You can edit a memorized report or chart only if you are the owner or a system administrator, 

even if the report or chart was made public. As the owner, you can change any parameter, 

including who owns it. The only field you cannot change is the report type.

Schedule Memorized Report Page 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Reports > All > Reports Center > Memorized > More Actions > Edit 
Schedule`


See the How to Get Here (on page 284) topic for a list of other ways to access memorized reports. 

Use the Schedule memorized report page to schedule a memorized report or chart to run 

automatically and routinely. You can schedule from either the Reports application or the 

application where the report or chart was originally memorized. 

This page contains three sections: 

• 
Filters: Use this section to filter the scheduled report by specific criteria. 

• 
Schedule: Use this section to define the report schedule. 

• 
Delivery options: Use this section to choose how you want to deliver and store the report.

Fields 

Memorized Reports List 

Edit (link)

memorized.

This column displays the report type (for example, Bank Register, Cash 

Balances, and so on). Different report types are configured with different 

parameters. 

Description 
This column displays the description that was used when the report was 

memorized. 

Last modified 
This column displays the date that the memorized report was last modified. 

Owner 
This column displays the name of the user who memorized the report. 

Public 
This column displays whether the memorized report is public or private. 

Run (link) 
Click this link to manually run a memorized report. 

Schedule (link) 
Click this link to schedule a memorized report to run automatically on a 

recurring basis. 

(Status) 
This column indicates whether or not the report is scheduled to run 

automatically. 

Delete (link) 
Click this link to delete a memorized report from the list. 

Edit Memorized Report Page 

The sections and fields on the Edit memorized report page vary depending on the report type. 

This topic covers only the fields in the General information section. Refer to the section of the 

RealPage Accounting help that corresponds with the report type for a field-by-field description of 

its filters and parameters. 

General Information Section 

Report type

list. 

Report 

description

Public 
Select this check box if you want to make the report available to all users in

Schedule Memorized Report Page 

Filters Section 

Department/grou

p 

Select a department or department group to filter the report for the 

department or department group. For example, you might want to filter an 

expense report for the Finance department.  

If the report already has filters, the system applies the department filter that 

you choose after the filters in the report’s definition. 

Location/group 
Select a location (or location group) to create a report that includes only the 

locations that you select.  

For example, you might want to filter a balance sheet for one location. If the 

report already has filters, the system applies the location after it applies the 

other filters in the report’s definition. 

As of date 
Leave this field blank to use the current date as the As of date for the report. 

The current date is the date that someone scheduled the system to run the 

report.  

If you type a date for the As of date, then the system always uses the date 

regardless of when the system runs the scheduled report.

reporting 

accounts labels

account set

the report with the reporting account set’s labels, and then select the 

Reporting account set whose labels you want to use. 

Memorize Report Parameters Page 

Name 
Type a name for the report that everyone can recognize. This field is 

required. 

Description 
Type a full description of the report.  

Public

and use your report parameters.

<!-- Page 308 -->
Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Schedule Memorized Report Page 

Schedule Section 

Start date 
Select or type the date on which you want the first memorized report to run. 

Typically, you type a date in the future. If you type a past date, the report 

executes after you save it. 

Ending on 
This setting determines when, if ever, the system stops generating the report 

or chart. Select one of the following options: 

• 
Never: Click this option to have the system generate reports until 

you instruct it to do otherwise. 

• 
Number of occurrences: Type the number of reports you want the 

system to generate. For example, to monitor a certain activity for 

three months, type a “3” here. 

• 
End date: To generate reports until a certain date, select End date, 

and then type or select the end date in the End on field. 

Recurrence 
This setting determines how often to generate the report. Select one of 

these options: 

• 
None: Click this option to issue only one report. 

• 
Days, Weeks, Months, Years: These four options work similarly. In all 

cases, you select the time and then the interval you want. For 

example, to generate a report every month, select Months, and then 

type “1” in the Every __ months field. If you leave this field blank, 

then the system defaults to “1.” 

End of month

check box if you want the system to disregard the Start date and run the 

report on the last day of the month instead.

#### Working Budget Report
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 312*

Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Working Budget Report 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budget Report`


Use the Working Budget report to analyze your budget information before you publish it. 
The Working Budget 
report is a standard report. If you are looking for information on financial reports, see Creating a Financial Report.

If you have a financial report with budget columns, you can also navigate into the Budget report, 

and from the Budget report to the Budget details page. There you can edit and update budget 

information, save, and see the changes reflected instantly in your financial report.  

In this report, you can: 

• 
Show total budget amounts across all periods.  

• 
Filter by location, department, and other dimensions.  

• 
Display columns for dimension values and select their display order.  

---

##### Customizing and Running the Working Budget Report
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 314*

Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Customizing and Running the Working Budget Report 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Working Budget Report`


To set up the Working Budget report: 

1. 
Click the Applications menu, select Forecasting & Planning, and then select the All tab.  

2. 
In the Reports column, in the Financial statements section, select Working Budget Report. 

The report parameters page opens. 

Filling In the Time Period Section 

Define the time period for the report. Either select the Budget period, or select a date range to 

identify the report’s coverage period. 

To run a report for a Budget period: 

• 
In the Budget period drop-down list, select the desired Budget period for the report. 

To run a report for a date range: 

• 
Specify the starting and ending dates to identify the report’s period of coverage.  
Type “ p ”in a date field to automatically insert the last day of the prior month. See 
Specifying Dates in Getting Started for a full list of shortcut keys related to entering dates.

Filling in the Filters Section 

1. 
In the Budget drop-down list, select the working budget. The report will list the accounts in 

the selected working budget. 

2. 
In the G/L account number selection section, select how you want to filter account numbers: 

• 
Range: Select this option to select a contiguous set of account numbers. Select the 

beginning and ending account numbers in the Account from and Account to 

drop-down lists. 

• 
Multiple accounts: Select this option if you want to select multiple, non-contiguous 

account numbers, and then click the Select accounts link to open the Select multiple 

accounts box. For more information, see Using the Select Multiple Accounts Box (on 

page 306). 

• 
All accounts: Select this option to include all account numbers in the report. 

3. 
In the Account group drop-down list, select an account group to filter by. You can also click 

the Find link to search for the desired account group. 

4. 
Select the Include statistical accounts in output check box if you want to include statistical 

account data in the Budget report. Clear the check box to exclude statistical account data. 

---


<!-- Page 315 -->
Chapter 5: Reports 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

5. 
Select the Include itemization rows check box to show itemization rows in the report. Clear 

the check to display period totals for the selected budget. 

6. 
In the Segments drop-down list, select one or more segments to include in the report. 
If the Segments drop
-down list does not appear, t
hen your system does not have the segments feature enabled(on page 407).

Setting Up the Dimension Filters 

Use the Dimension filters section to select the dimensions to filter the report by. 

1. 
To filter by dimension report structure, select the desired dimension report structure in the 

Dimension report structure drop-down list. 

2. 
The remaining fields in the Dimension filters section vary from company to company. Typical 

supported dimensions include Department, Location, Customer, Vendor, and Employee. 

Select the desired dimension(s) or dimension group(s) from the drop-down lists. 
You can select multiple locations in this report. See Using the Multi-Select Box for more 
information. 

• 
Select the Include subs check box if you want to include data from children of the 

selected dimension. 

• 
Some dimensions have a type, such as a Customer type or Employee type. Select a 

type if you want to include a specific type of dimension when you run the report.  
Dimension types vary by company. You can add and remove customer types in the Accounts Receivable application. You can add and remove vendor types in the Accounts Payable application.

Defining the Report Format 

Use the Format section to format the report output. 

1. 
In the Show dimension values field, select the dimension values you want to appear in the 

report. See Using the Multi-Select Box for more information. The dimension values will 

display in the report in the order you selected. 

2. 
In the Place total column drop-down list, select where to display the Total column in the 

report: 

• 
Left: Displays the column to the left of the budget period. 

• 
Right: Displays the column to the right of the budget period. 

• 
Hide: Hides the column. 

3. 
Select the Use CSV importable format check box if you want to format the report as a CSV 

template. This allows you to export your custom report to a CSV file and then use it to 

import data back into your working budget (on page 175). 

---

CSV from the

- down 
button.

4. 
In the Sort by drop-down list, select the dimension or segment by which you want to sort 

the report data. 

Using the Select Multiple Accounts Box 

Use the Select multiple accounts box to include multiple, non-contiguous G/L account numbers 

in a report. You can add account numbers to your selection individually or add a range of account 

numbers. 

Adding Individual G/L Accounts 

To add G/L accounts individually: 

1. 
In the Add G/L account drop-down list, select the desired account number. 

2. 
Click the Add button next to the field. 

The account appears in the Selected accounts list. 

3. 
Continue adding account numbers as needed, and then click Save. 

Adding a Range of G/L Accounts 

To add a range of G/L accounts: 

1. 
In the From account drop-down list, select the starting account number in the desired 

range. 

2. 
In the To account drop-down list, select the ending account number in the desired range. 

---

3. 
Click the Add button next to the To account drop-down list. 

The account range appears in the Selected accounts list as a single row. 

4. 
Continue adding account ranges as needed, and then click Save. 

Reordering Selected Accounts

 and 
 arrows in the Selected accounts list to reorder the account numbers. 

Removing Selected Accounts

Click the 
 icon.

#### Account Groups Hierarchy Report
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 320*

Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Account Groups Hierarchy Report 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > General Ledger > All > Account Groups Hierarchy`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Account Groups Hierarchy`


Use the Account Group Hierarchy report to display a graphical representation of the hierarchy of 

your account groups, which includes member account groups and their accounts. The lists of 

account groups and accounts are sorted in ascending alphabetical order. If an account group has 

no members, only the accounts are displayed which helps you identify groups that have not been 

mapped to a group. 
The Account Group Hierarchy 
repor t is a standard report. If you are looking for information on 
financial reports, see Creating a Financial Report.

The main headings in this report are taken from the account name as entered in the G/L account 

group information page or the G/L account information page. This is different from other 

financial reports, which typically use the heading title, and then the name if no title is specified. 

The reason the name is used here is so that you can more easily compare the names in this report 

to the names in the Account groups list. 

The divisions in the report are differentiated by whether they are top-level groups that contain 

other member groups, and if they are groups that contain only accounts or categories. Any group 

that contains only accounts or categories is displayed after the top-level groups. The hierarchical 


> **Navigation Path:** `organization of account groups is controlled via General Ledger > All > Financial report`


structures > [member link] > Account group members. 

If your company was created with a Quick Start Financials setup template: 

• 
The report operates slightly differently for companies that use a Quick Start Financials setup 

template in that the setup template creates a set of system account groups in addition to 

your own account groups. 

• 
If your company was initially created with a setup template, a set of standard account 

groups was copied to your company. Consequently, a filter lets you choose to view the 

system account groups or the user-defined ones that your company created. You can 

navigate into user-defined account groups by clicking a link on the report, but you can’t 

navigate on system-generated account groups. 

---



##### Customizing and Running the Account Groups Hierarchy Report
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 321*

Chapter 5: Reports 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Customizing and Running the Account Groups Hierarchy Report 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > General Ledger > All > Reports > Financial Statements > Account 
Groups Hierarchy`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Reports > Account Groups Hierarchy`


To customize the Account Groups Hierarchy report: 

1. 
In the Applications menu, select either General Ledger or Forecasting & Planning, and then 

click the All tab.  

2. 
In the Reports section, select the Account groups hierarchy option. 

Filling In the Reporting Accounts Labels Section 

If you enabled reporting account labels in the General Ledger, you can substitute the account 

labels with the reporting account set’s labels in the report. For more information, see Reporting 

Account Sets. 

1. 
To enable reporting account labels, select the Substitute with reporting accounts labels 

check box. 

2. 
In the Reporting account set drop-down list, select the reporting account set whose labels 

you want to substitute into the report.  

3. 
If you prefer to choose the reporting account set each time that you run the report, then 

select the Prompt on run check box. When you select this option, the system asks you for 

the reporting account set every time you run the report. 

Filling In the Filters Section 

1. 
In the Select drop-down list, select whether to filter the report by User defined account 

groups or System account groups. These choices are available for companies that were 

initially created with a setup template that automatically generated categories mapped to 

account groups and standard financial reports based on the account groups. 

2. 
You can filter on an Account group or a range of account groups. 

• 
To filter on an account group, select an account group in the Account group from 

drop-down list. If you want to filter on a range, select the first account group in the 

range of account groups. 

• 
To filter on a range of account groups, select the account group at the end of the 

range in the Account group to drop-down list. 

3. 
You can filter on an Account number or a range of account numbers. 

• 
To filter on an account number, select an account number in the Account number 

from drop-down list. If you want to filter on a range, select the first account number in 

the range of account numbers. 

---


<!-- Page 322 -->
Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

• 
To filter on a range of account numbers, select the account number at the end of the 

range in the Account number to drop-down list. 

4. 
You can filter on a Category, or a range of categories. 

• 
To filter on a category, select a category in the Category from drop-down list. If you 

want to filter on a range, select the first category in the range of categories. 

• 
To filter on a range of categories, select the category at the end of the range in the 

Category to drop-down list. 
Categories are available only to those companies that were set up with a QuickStart template.

Filling In the Format Section 

Select the desired page orientation. Portrait is the default layout. 

• 
Portrait: Display or print the report pages vertically 

• 
Landscape: Display or print the report pages horizontally 

Filling In the Titles Section and Run the Report 

Titles, subtitles, and footers apply only to the PDF version of this report (click Print to get the PDF 

version). 

To add a title, subtitle, and footer to the report: 

1. 
Type the title in the Report title 1 box. 

This title displays in large type at the top of the page, directly under your company name. 

You can type over any default name that the system may have automatically supplied in this 

box. 

2. 
Type the subtitle in the Report title 2 box. 

This subtitle displays in smaller type, directly beneath the main title. 

3. 
Type the footer text in the Footer text box. Footer text displays at the bottom center of the 

report. 
You can use up to80 characters, including spaces, in each title, subtitle, and footer.

4. 
Click the View button to run the report. 

---

#### Chart of Accounts Report
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 325*

Chapter 5: Reports 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Chart of Accounts Report 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > General Ledger > All > Chart of Accounts`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Chart of Accounts`


Use the Chart of Accounts report to view a filtered list of accounts. This report contains the same 

information as the Accounts list in General Ledger; however, you can’t edit the information in this 

report. 
The Chart of Accounts 
report is a standard report. If you are looking for information on financial reports, see Creating a Financial Report.

To examine the way your accounts are mapped into groups, run the Account Groups Hierarchy 

report. The Account Groups Hierarchy report shows you the hierarchical structure—that is, which 

accounts are members of which groups and which groups are members of other 

groups—whereas the Chart of Accounts report is a flat listing of all accounts. 

---

##### Customizing and Running the Chart of Accounts Report
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 327*

Chapter 5: Reports 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Customizing and Running the Chart of Accounts Report 


> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > General Ledger > All > Reports > Financial Statements > Chart of 
Accounts`



> **Navigation Path:** `Applications > Forecasting & Planning > All > Reports > Chart of Accounts`


To customize the Chart of Accounts report: 

1. 
Open the General Ledger menu.  

2. 
In the Reports section, select the Chart of accounts option. 

Filling In the Filters Section 

You can leave the filter fields blank, which is the same as specifying “All,” or you can enter values 

that limit the list so it is more focused. You can continue to modify the filter criteria and other 

settings until you are satisfied with the results. However, you must click View or another option 

each time you change the settings. 

To set filters to refine your report results: 

1. 
Select the account type you want to include in the report: 

• 
G/L Accounts 

• 
Balance Sheet Accounts 

• 
Income Statement Accounts 

• 
Statistical Accounts 

2. 
Select the range of accounts you want in the report. Select accounts for the Account from 

and Account to from the drop-down lists. 

3. 
To include accounts for which you have required departments, select the Required 

departments check box. 

4. 
To include accounts for which you have required locations, select the Required locations 

check box. 

5. 
Select the Period end closing type from the drop-down list. 

6. 
Select the type of Normal balance for your report.  

The normal balance indicates the type of balance that this account is expected to have, 

based on how it was classified when it was set up, which is shown in the chart of accounts 

and in the accounts list. 

7. 
Select the status of the accounts to include in the report. To include both active and inactive 

accounts, select All. 

The following table describes each item in the Filters section of this report settings page: 

---


<!-- Page 328 -->
Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

Account type 
Select the type of accounts to include. G/L accounts, 

Balance sheet accounts, Income Statement accounts, 

or Statistical accounts. 

Account from 

Account to 

Select the beginning and ending account numbers to 

include in the report. 

Require department 
Select this check box to include only accounts that 

require departments. 

Require location 
Select this check box to include only accounts that 

require locations. 

Period end closing type 
Select All, Closing, Non-closing, Close to accounts. 

Closing entries, or closing journal entries, are entries 

that are made at the end of an accounting period to 

zero out the accounts so that you start over in the 

next period. Some types of accounts don’t need to 

close. For example, statistical accounts are often 

non-closing. Close to accounts are those accounts 

that one account closes into. For example, a 

temporary account for petty cash might close to a 

permanent account for general expenses. 

Normal balance 
Include all normal balance accounts, or only those 

with Credit or Debit normal balances. 

The normal balance indicates the type of balance that 

this account is expected to have, based on how it was 

classified when it was set up, which is shown in the 

chart of accounts and in the accounts list. 

Status 
Include all accounts or limit the report to only active 

or inactive account. 

Filling In the Format Section 

Use this section to specify whether to hide your company logo when displaying the PDF version 

of this report and to change the page layout of the report. 

The following table describes each item in the Format section of this report settings page: 

1. 
Select the Page orientation. 

• 
Portrait: Display or print the report pages vertically. 

•

Portrait is the defaultpage orientation.

2. 
To remove your company logo in the PDF version of this report, select the Hide logo check 

box. 

Filling In the Titles Section 

Use this section to add a title, subtitle, and footer to your report. Titles, subtitles, and footers 

apply only to the PDF version of this report (click Print to get the PDF version). 

The system completes the Report title 1 field with the system-assigned name of the report. This 

title appears in large type at the top of the page directly below your company name. You can 

leave the title as the default or change it.  

1. 
To change the title, type over the default name that the system automatically supplied. 

2.

3. 
In the Footer text field, type the text that you want to appear in the footer of the report. 

Footer text appears at the bottom of the report. 
You can use up to 80 characters, including spaces, in each title, subtitle, and footer.

4. 
Click the View button to run the report.  

What’s Next?  

After you run the report, you can also either email the report to others or return to the report 

setup page to refine the results.

• 
Email the report to others. Click the Email button.

Return to the main report page and refine the report further. Click the Customize button.

##### Buttons
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 330*

Reports 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide

Chart of Accounts Columns 

Click any link to navigate into the details. The default Chart of Accounts report contains the 

following columns: 

Columns 

Account no 
This is the unique account number. 

Title 
This is the name of the account. 

Type 
This is the BAL (balance sheet) or INC (income statement). 

Balance 
This is the normal balance, either DB (debit) or CR (credit). 

Closes into 
This is the account that this account number closes into. 

Require department 
If Yes, then this department is required to run the report. 

Require location 
If Yes, then this location is required to run the report. 

Buttons 

Customize 
Opens the report’s customization page to change filters and time periods 

and to exclude specific types of data. 

The Customize button appears only after viewing a report or ledger.

View 
Opens the report in your browser. 

Print 
Displays the report in printable PDF format so you can print it or save it on 

your computer. 

Process & store 
Generates the report and stores it for later access. 

Available formats include HTML, printable (PDF), Excel, CSV 

(comma-separated value), and Text. You can store reports in the system, 

and then access them with the My stored reports option in the menu.  

Email 
Sends the report as an email message. Click to open the Email report box, 

and then fill in the To field with the recipient’s email address. 
This button is not available for all reports.

Add to dashboard 
Adds the report to a dashboard. 

Select the dashboard that you want to add it to, type a name for the 

report, and then type a description of the report.  

Select the Public check box to make the report available on a dashboard. 
This option is not available for all reports.

Memorize 
Memorizes (that is, saves) the report in the system so you can refer to it 

later or modify and re-run it. 
Reports that you memorize can be scheduled to run on a recurring basis and can be automatically emailed.

Export 
Exports the report to a separate file that you can then distribute or save on 

your computer. 
The file formats for exporting report data vary by report.

Select 
Opens the multi-select box, where you can select items to filter by. 

In the multi-select box, select the items that you want to include in the 

report.  
The option to multi-select items is not available for all reports.

Setting Up Dimensions for Forecasting & Planning 

If your company is configured to use dimensions, then all of your company’s dimensions are available in the 

Forecasting & Planning application. You must have administrator-level permissions to add and manage 

dimensions. 

The Forecasting & Planning application works with existing dimension data that you have added using other 

applications in the RealPage Financial Suite. These dimension menu options allow you to add and manage the 

data from this application as well. 

To find out which dimensions are available to use in Forecasting & Planning: 

• 
In the Forecasting & Planning application, click the Setup tab.  

The menu includes a list of dimensions that your company has enabled. The list may vary if your company 

created custom dimensions or enabled dimensions beyond those that RealPage supports.

##### Adding Vendor (Supplier) Records
*Source: `RP Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide.pdf`, Page 343*

Chapter 7: Setting Up Dimensions for Forecasting & Planning 

© 2025 RealPage, Inc. 
RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 

Adding Vendor (Supplier) Records 

See the How to Get Here (on page 331) topic for a list of ways to access the Vendors list. 

Adding Vendor Records 

Add vendor records when you want to pay A/P invoices.  

When you add a vendor, you can type key descriptive information about that vendor and select 

the payment method that the vendor prefers, such as by check, credit card, or ACH (electronic 

payment). 
If you enabled the Single Scan Uploadfeature in your preferences and have previously 
downloaded the Dynamsoft scanning software, you may be prompted to update the software when you add a new scannable record such as an A/P invoice, vendor, purchase order, service contract, or document. When this occurs, download the new software; there is no need to uninstall the previous version.

To add a basic vendor record: 

1. 
In the ID field, type a unique identifier for the vendor record. 
Use a character as a prefix to your ID; for example, V0001 for a vendor ID.

When you save the record, the system converts the vendor ID to all uppercase to prevent 

duplication. You can prevent this conversion by clearing the Set IDs to uppercase check 

box. For information, see Disabling Vendor ID Capitalization. 

2. 
In the Name field, type the vendor’s name. The system completes the Print as field in the 

Primary Contact section. 

3. 
Select the One-time use check box to designate the vendor as a one-time or occasional use 

vendor that you don’t want to include in your standard vendor lists. An example of a 

one-time use of a vendor record is when you issue a customer’s deposit refund. 
These vendors won’t appear in the 
Vendors (Suppliers)list unless you select the 
Include 

one-timeoption at the top of the list.

4. 
Select the Corporate vendor check box as needed. When you add an invoice for a corporate 

vendor, the system marks the invoice as a corporate invoice and prevents it from appearing 

in Site Experience. Also, corporate vendors do not appear in Site Experience. 

5. 
Select the This vendor is an owner check box when the vendor is also the owner of the 

company. 

6. 
In the Status list, select the Active option if you want vendors to have A/P invoices entered 

and payments issued. You can also select the Inactive option to prevent A/P invoices from 

being entered and payment issued. 

---


<!-- Page 344 -->
Setting Up Dimensions for Forecasting & Planning 

RealPage Accounting Forecasting & Planning User Guide 
© 2025 RealPage, Inc.

7. 
Select the appropriate Storefront supplier code. This code is the vendor’s code for use with 

the Storefront application associated with Spend Management. If your company does not 

use Spend Management, then this option does not appear. 

8. 
If your company has purchased and installed Spend Management, the Require PO – Spend 

check box appears. Select this option when your company requires that you create a manual 

or online purchase order before creating an online A/P invoice on behalf of the vendor. If a 

purchase order is not available to match with the A/P invoice, then the user cannot submit 

the A/P invoice for the vendor. 

9. 
Select the Exclude from Full Service check box to prevent the vendor from syncing with 

Invoice processing full service. You can enable syncing again at any time. 

10. 
Select the Preferred vendor check box as appropriate. 

11. 
Select the OneSite resident check box if the vendor is a resident in a OneSite-managed 

property. 

12. 
Select the Sync to external systems check box to allow RealPage Accounting to sync the 

vendor record with Procore or other external systems. 
This field appears only if you have configured the Procore integration in Job Cost & Reserves.

13. 
Select the RequirePO check box when the vendor requires a purchase order. The A/P 

invoice must be matched to a purchase order before you save and post it. 
This field does not apply to actions performed in the RealPage Financial Suite system. The RequirePO check box appears only when your system is integrated with OPSTechnology. 
Whenthe RequirePO check box is selected, it causes the OPS system to validate the 
purchase order record in OPS.

14. 
In the Contract agreement field, select or type the agreement date. 

15. 
Click the Small bus Select button, and then click 
 Add for each small business type that 

the vendor qualifies for a special designation: 

• 
Disabled veteran 

• 
Veteran-owned 

• 
Women-owned 

Filling In the Tax Information Section 

The fields in this section vary based on the country where the vendor is located.  

1. 
In the Country field, type the country where the vendor is located.  

2. 
In the Tax ID field, type the company’s tax identification number or the individual’s Social 

Security Number.

want it to appear on checks. Revise the Print as name if necessary. If this vendor requires 

joint checks, then fill in the names of the two required signers. This field is required. 

3.

10. 
In the URL field, type the URL of the company’s website. This information is for your 

reference only and is not used by the system. 

11. 
Select the Exclude From the Company Contact List check box to exclude the vendor’s 

primary contact from being included in the company’s contact list. 

12.

Payment Information
